# OpenClaw v2026.10.1

AI agents and tools can read these release notes as [plain Markdown](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/openclaw/openclaw/main/CHANGELOG/2026.10.1.md).

OpenClaw v2026.10.1 helps agents [find useful installed skills](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills#search-installed-skills) when they need them and read the full instructions separately, without loading the whole library into their prompt. Compatible, opt-in [MCP Apps](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/mcp/apps#plugin-extensions) can open beside a conversation, with forms, selected context and file viewing or editing within the access you grant. You can also [keep verified full backups](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/backups) on another disk or in Cloudflare R2, with optional schedules and restoration into a staging directory. Set up the destination explicitly and choose encryption: `none` and any archive kept with `--output` are plaintext. Keep the recovery marker and, for encrypted backups, the passphrase.

**Release scale:** 2391 pull requests, 601 direct commits, and 358 contributors.

## Installation and Onboarding

Setup changes cover interrupted sign-in, mobile pairing behind a custom web address, and installations using Bun or cloud workers.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Setup and sign-in recovery">

You can correct an invalid LM Studio or llama.cpp address during setup. After a Gateway restart, the web setup can also reload saved settings and let you continue once you verify the selected model. Rerunning setup preserves an existing trusted-proxy sign-in policy unless you explicitly choose another mode. Combining that policy with Tailscale Funnel is refused, so choose password sign-in explicitly or leave public exposure off.

General configuration examples no longer impose a ten-minute limit on an entire agent run. If you copied that setting into your own configuration, review the [agent timeout guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/agent-loop#timeouts) and remove or change it yourself.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Recreate a fully deleted agent with its previous ID through guided `agents add`, using copied or new sign-in credentials. Recreation remains blocked while deletion cleanup is pending. [#143991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143991), thanks to [@Giannoulakos](https://github.com/Giannoulakos), [@ioannis-giannoulakos](https://github.com/ioannis-giannoulakos), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@Arslan-Mehmood1](https://github.com/Arslan-Mehmood1).
- Configuration examples drop an accidental 600-second limit on the whole agent run. Existing copied configurations need a manual change, and the runtime default is unchanged. [#158513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158513), thanks to [@HunterStile](https://github.com/HunterStile), [@100yenadmin](https://github.com/100yenadmin) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Starting a Gateway directly or in a container now prevents a second instance from sharing state already owned by a running Gateway, even on another port. Startup can wait up to five minutes before reporting the conflict. [#160193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160193), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@grtninja](https://github.com/grtninja) and [@R3NK0R](https://github.com/R3NK0R).
- Correct an invalid local-model server address in either web or terminal setup. LM Studio and external llama.cpp addresses accept HTTP or HTTPS without embedded credentials, and shorthand addresses still work. [#160720](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160720), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The setup welcome and options follow your selected Simplified or Traditional Chinese language even when it differs from the browser language or translations are still loading. [#162913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162913), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Multi-agent documentation uses the correct configuration shape and makes ownership and routing explicit. If you manually replace the binary while keeping a legacy configuration, run `openclaw doctor --fix` before startup. Normal updates already run Doctor through their backup flow. [#163475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163475), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Web model setup can recover after a Gateway restart by successfully refreshing saved settings. A saved model needs explicit verification before continuing. If the refresh fails, the setup guard stays in place and offers **Check again**. [#164602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164602), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fresh installations avoid irrelevant Matrix migration work when Matrix is unconfigured and a complete scan finds no legacy state. Configured Matrix installations and incomplete scans retain the migration checks. [#165209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165209), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- If replacement sign-in credentials fail to activate, the previous saved credential is restored before reconnecting. [cd75f44](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cd75f44f9cd3fb2476beb313f87d8b8acd50f2f5), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Interactive Copilot setup recognizes a saved token or secret reference already authorized for the same host domain. Secret references stay references, and changing domains requires a fresh login. [332e4d3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/332e4d3ec02df53b18003155fe44a0a102b383e1), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Setup reruns preserve trusted-proxy sign-in and its trust policy unless you explicitly change them. Completion checks use the configured local Gateway and its local password, preserving secret references. An incompatible Tailscale Funnel choice is refused before rewriting the policy. [1f74f3c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1f74f3cf39b065f89a4b5bffc63e5d9dd53fcc92), thanks to [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Mobile and cloud pairing">

New mobile pairing codes preserve the [configured Control UI path](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-gateway), so Settings opens at the right address when the web interface lives under a custom path. Affected existing clients need a new code and must re-pair.

Cloud pairing can finish after a download runs past ten minutes while enrollment remains active. Interrupted downloads can also keep making progress through retries. The revised [cloud-worker setup command](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/setup-and-bundle-installation#the-setup-command) handles first-boot package locks and an existing nvm installation. Adopt the revised example in saved setup scripts to use those changes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Cloud-worker pairing can complete after downloads exceed ten minutes while enrollment is still active. The ten-minute lifetime of generic setup tokens is unchanged. [#161552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161552), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New mobile QR and setup codes retain the Control UI mount path in setup and join addresses, fixing Settings links that returned 404. Affected clients need a new code and must re-pair. [#161620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161620), thanks to [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez) and [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Cloud bootstrap retries continue while the download gains bytes, avoiding an early stop after several interruptions that still made progress. Three consecutive transient failures with no progress end the attempt, and the existing overall deadlines still apply. [#162408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162408), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Preparing and checking cloud-node packages no longer holds up the Gateway's main thread, so it can handle other work during that step. [#163446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163446), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Interrupted worker-bundle downloads resume from bytes retained during the same installation, then check the complete size and hash before use. Three consecutive failures with no progress stop the attempt, and cancellation or failure removes the temporary download. [#164114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164114), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The revised Debian and Ubuntu cloud setup example waits for first-boot initialization when Node needs installation and gives package-manager locks up to ten minutes to clear. Existing saved setup profiles need the revised script. [#164133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164133), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The revised cloud setup example repairs the Node version selected by nvm instead of installing an APT copy that nvm would hide. It also reports the selected path and version. Existing saved scripts need the revised example. [#164613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164613), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Installation packages and runtime choices">

Supported Bun-only installations get fixes for first install, the `openclaw` launcher and native dependencies. On macOS and Linux, eligible global installs can launch without Node, but older published updaters still need a supported upgrade route. npm installations also avoid native binaries for unrelated platforms. Existing oversized installs are not pruned automatically, and an older updater that refuses the package may need a supported manual reinstall.

The [macOS VM guide](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/f05312877512a29b216ae233c4a2ee9f535b1f89/docs/install/macos-vm.md) now walks through the [installer](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/installer), Node and npm setup, and Gateway service installation before headless use. Check the [Node installation guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/node) for platform requirements. Reported CLI operation on older macOS versions does not make them supported.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- The fresh macOS VM guide uses the existing installer to provide Node and npm, then directs you to stop the foreground Gateway with Ctrl+C, run `openclaw gateway install`, and check status before headless setup. [#160239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160239), thanks to [@samadarsh](https://github.com/samadarsh).
- Source-install instructions without Corepack now select the pnpm version pinned by the checkout, removing its integrity suffix before installation. The required install scripts and optional dependencies remain part of the instructions. [#160513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160513), thanks to [@Irish-Joseph](https://github.com/Irish-Joseph).
- Optional macOS DNS setup explains when its Homebrew-prefix lookup hits the existing 15-second timeout or is terminated. The messages clarify a failed step without adding a new timeout. [#160797](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160797), thanks to [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- When a normal source build falls back to prebuilt Canvas assets, it now rejects an incomplete renderer set instead of accepting a bundle missing a required version. [#161390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161390), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- First install on a Bun-only machine skips Node checks when Node is absent or unusable. This still requires the supported explicit Bun 1.4 or newer launcher, and older published updaters do not gain Bun-only update support retroactively. [#161512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161512), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible trusted Bun-only global installs on macOS and Linux can run plain `openclaw` without Node using the selected Bun executable. The OpenClaw command still needs to be on your PATH. Reinstall to refresh the launcher, or invoke the installed entry through Bun and use `doctor --fix` to approve its repair. [#162541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162541), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Bun native-dependency repair preserves existing packages and warns when a repair is unavailable. Codex startup retains useful missing-file, permission and architecture errors, while malformed JSON produces a redacted warning and no longer blocks later valid messages. [#162559](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162559), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- macOS documentation distinguishes Node's official macOS 13.5 build target from reported CLI and Gateway operation on macOS 12 with Node 24. Older macOS remains unsupported and untested, and native binaries and the app have separate requirements. [#163324](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163324), thanks to [@NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi), [@xXG0DLessXx](https://github.com/xXG0DLessXx) and [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- npm packages bundle worker files in an archive so their file count no longer trips the updater's entry limit, while retaining worker support. [#163985](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163985), thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- npm installs filter optional native packages by platform, reducing the disk used by unrelated binaries. Existing oversized installations are not automatically pruned, and an older updater that rejects the package may require a supported manual reinstall. [#165997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165997), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@tommyfive](https://github.com/TommyFive) and [@kenmege](https://github.com/Kenmege).
- Published packages now include the generator and helper files needed by the prepare hook, fixing a package-integrity failure during Docker seed installation. [98421de](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/98421de805fb68652bcd5d0877116a469255590e), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Web UI

The Control UI adds ways to organize conversations, work with replies and media, and keep using chat through loading or connection changes.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Finding and organizing conversations">

[Snooze](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) moves eligible active conversations out of the way while their work continues. Wake times, Undo and the Snoozed filter make them easy to return to, while new interaction, completion or pinning can bring them back early. Saved-draft markers help you find unfinished messages, and confirmed archive or restore actions update the loaded sidebar immediately.

Search previews now show basic formatting and highlight matching URLs. Title search handles punctuation and spacing, and the final term in a message search can be a word prefix. Online also offers All and Running views with sorting by presence, running work, accessible open chats or name.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Enabled sidebar progress can show tool icons on a second line while retaining readable activity text and accessible names. [#160066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160066). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large sidebars avoid repeated work on unrelated updates while activity subtitles continue refreshing. [#160876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160876). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- The navigation rail uses the configured agent identity, with a generic fallback when its name is empty. [#160941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160941). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Snooze temporarily hides eligible active conversations while their work continues. Presets, wake times, Undo and the Snoozed or All filters make them accessible; new interaction, user-facing completion or pinning can wake them early. [#161960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161960). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Switching through large rosters avoids unnecessary archive lookups for ordinary and restored conversations. [#161995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161995). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved-draft pencil markers include text, attachments, replies and goals after reload or a fresh tab. Incognito is excluded, and unavailable browser storage falls back to the current tab. [#162049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162049). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Message-search previews show basic inline formatting and highlighted URL matches while keeping links and images passive. [#162279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162279). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation-title search normalizes punctuation and spacing, and message search supports a prefix in the final query term. Earlier message terms still need complete words. [#162517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162517). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Offscreen sidebar indicators pause their animation while sessions continue working. [#162905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162905). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Online offers All and Running filters, plus presence, running-count, open-chat-count and name sorting. Counts cover accessible open chats rather than lifetime history. [#163664](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163664). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski) and [@jesse-merhi](https://github.com/jesse-merhi).

**Bug fixes**

- Reading a conversation clears its unread marker without making it more recent. Marking it unread can still change its position. [#158577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158577). Thanks [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Channel `/new` and `/reset` keep custom conversation groups. [#161066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161066). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@catnipluss](https://github.com/catnipluss) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Conversation links discard old or late titles after Gateway, profile or connection changes while the underlying link remains usable. [#161433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161433). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sidebar Reset restores visible Filters and Display defaults while preserving hidden all-agent preferences. [#161437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161437). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Confirmed Archive, Restore and Undo update the loaded sidebar immediately, without waiting for a separate event or page reload. [#162208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162208). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation titles no longer gain repeated translated prefixes when those prefixes contain punctuation, and hiding the prefix works too. [#162331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162331). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session-only users avoid catalog permission errors, and revoked or late catalog results stop appearing as current. [#162944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162944). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- New Session ends a stalled model-catalog wait after a shared twenty-second deadline, explains that no session was created and retains the draft for retry. This deadline covers catalog waiting rather than the full workspace setup or first answer. [#165989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165989). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi) and [@dprev](https://github.com/dprev).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Writing messages and viewing media">

Replies use a compact Replying to author line that can take you back to the original message. Queued-image previews and readable comment editors help you review input. When you enter characters with an input method, confirming a character no longer also saves or sends the unfinished message. New attachments added to progress updates stay in the conversation after temporary files are removed, including when an interrupted run is recovered.

Assistant replies can offer [YouTube cards](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat#youtube-videos) with a thumbnail, Play button, start time and external watch link. Thumbnails contact Google before you press Play. Strict mode, narrow cards or provider restrictions may require opening YouTube separately. Permitted native local audio and video also gain [inline playback and seeking](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/media-playback) up to 4 GiB, subject to browser codec and resource limits.

Saved Draft and Unsent rows put recovery above the composer with previews and Restore or Delete controls. Recovery never sends automatically. If a message may already have been sent, check its original conversation before restoring it; deleting a saved recovery copy does not delete sent history.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Pasted-text and grouped-comment attachments load near the visible conversation or when focused, keeping keyboard focus as their details arrive. [#156277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156277). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Context-usage warnings mark estimated values as approximate. [#159549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159549). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reply and copy controls follow hover, keyboard focus and touch selection without shifting the message footer. [#160612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160612). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Selected-text comment pins avoid unnecessary repositioning when new content streams below their source. [#160799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160799). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scroll to latest shows hover feedback. [#161009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161009). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Queued messages show a small preview of their first image. [#161459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161459). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Conversation image previews gain faint borders that follow the theme. [#161478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161478). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Adjacent updates from the same outside conversation fold into expandable rows. Search and reply navigation reveal the original messages, with their times and available source links. [#161656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161656). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reply-context help and cold-history loading labels use the existing twenty translated locales. [#161664](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161664).
- Saved recovery shows meaningful previews, attachment and message counts, and a source conversation when known. Restoration moves the saved input into an empty eligible chat without sending; uncertain delivery remains paused for checking in the original chat. Confirmed deletion removes the displayed saved copies and files, not sent history. [#163717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163717). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Permitted native local audio and video can play inline and seek with authentication up to 4 GiB. Browser codec and resource limits still apply; remote-workspace large-media delivery is excluded. Rejected unsafe media links show Media not attached without exposing the URL. [#164137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164137). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Assistant replies can show YouTube thumbnail cards with Play, supported start times and an external watch link. Thumbnails contact Google before Play; strict mode, narrow cards and provider restrictions can require external playback. [#164576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164576). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Replying to uses the original author's avatar and name instead of repeating a quoted excerpt. Selecting it returns to and highlights the original, opening collapsed work or clearing search when needed. Unknown authors are not guessed, and unavailable originals are not clickable. [ed9d2c1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ed9d2c1605c32e6c960bfc7a491038f2013420c6). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).

**Bug fixes**

- Use device camera remains available after a denied or failed preview so the browser can handle another camera attempt. [#160359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160359). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A restricted composer explains why sending is unavailable and retains its draft and attachments. Queued sends recheck access for the relevant agent. [#160977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160977). Thanks [@stevenlee-oai](https://github.com/stevenlee-oai).
- Short chat panes and installed-app canvases keep the editor and Send or Stop controls reachable. Very short panes may hide the transcript to make room. [#161134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161134). Thanks [@hxy91819](https://github.com/hxy91819).
- Confirming composed characters keeps the chat draft, annotations and rename input from acting on that same Enter key. [#161302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161302). Thanks [@NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@stbtyx747](https://github.com/stbtyx747).
- Collapsed completed work keeps widgets and browser previews in their execution order. [#161473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161473). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- System and danger notices have balanced spacing in desktop and touch conversations. [#161479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161479). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Newly staged inbound images retain their display references when history is reopened or reloaded, without exposing private workspace paths. Already-damaged older transcripts are not repaired. [#161842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161842). Thanks [@brandon-julio-t](https://github.com/brandon-julio-t) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Your own replies keep Original message unavailable visible when the source cannot be found. [#162040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162040). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Replying to stays with its completed work and answer after resumptions or partial history. Earlier failures remain visible without marking a later successful answer as failed. [#162322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162322). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Saved OpenAI safety refusals that contain only an older code now display their explanation and guidance beside a partial answer. [#162469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162469). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Successfully queued, unchanged attachments leave the composer while newly edited text and files remain. Storage or connection changes prevent stale cleanup. [#162898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162898). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Generated media from retained rewind branches remains available when returning to that branch after file cleanup. Retention uses disk until the branch is removed, and already-deleted files cannot be restored. [#163043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163043). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
- Add to chat comment editors stay readable while scrolling in desktop and mobile create or edit views. [#163772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163772). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Saved queue copies disappear when loaded history proves the exact message was delivered in the same conversation and recorded session. Remaining Draft or Unsent rows retain Restore, Delete and possible-send warnings; recovery never sends automatically. [#164486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164486). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Confirming composed characters keeps queued-message editing open and preserves the Side chat draft without sending. A later deliberate shortcut performs the intended save or send. [#164560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164560). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst).
- New authored progress attachments from session-bound agent runs remain in history after temporary files disappear or the page reloads, including recovered runs. Previously lost files are not restored. [#165275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165275). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Queued messages and their remove controls remain visible when a plugin replaces, delegates or fails to mount the composer. [1571690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/15716905f62344c36b975583c5ab4e6d96b41052). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Loading and reconnecting">

Normal agent preparation now shows Starting up and loads chat and sidebar data automatically when ready, even when preparation takes several minutes. Long conversations avoid repeated local processing, and large Claude CLI conversations use ordinary pages and backscroll. The latter still builds an initial index, and an unavailable native transcript can prevent loading even when local history is readable.

During connection loss, cached conversation content, drafts and queues remain available. Prepared Chat and New Session pages can reopen when the browser is explicitly offline and their cache and retained access information are complete. Widgets can retain local state through a temporary disconnect, with server actions waiting for revalidation; reloading does not retain that widget state. See [offline and reconnect behavior](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect).

Cached conversations and drafts are readable to anyone who can use that browser profile. Older unowned drafts or queued messages require explicit recovery review and are never sent automatically. Clearing site data can remove those saved copies, and older UI versions cannot read newly saved queues after an upgrade. Reconnect bursts prioritize getting connections established, so the first session lists may take longer to arrive.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Sessions avoid unnecessary refreshes when their effective settings are unchanged. Changed values and resolved secrets still refresh them. [#155300](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155300). Thanks [@azuretek](https://github.com/azuretek), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc) and [@jbfranklin](https://github.com/jbfranklin).
- Checking for a pending question avoids rereading history that cannot contain it. [#159556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159556). Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored) and [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez).
- Cold remote conversations can show the transcript earlier, and permitted favicon reuse avoids repeat downloads within the current credentials. [#160293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160293). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Bun-based Gateways reuse their state reader during startup to reduce congestion that could delay sign-in and conversation lists. [#161040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161040). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New conversations avoid repeated background reads when their parent is missing while showing the new row and slash commands promptly. [#161442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161442). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Short history pages avoid parsing older messages that are already indexed. [#161638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161638). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Initial Control UI loading makes fewer JavaScript requests, with remote desktop available when opened. [#161648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161648). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Signed-in startup avoids premature sign-in downloads and duplicate matching self-avatar requests. Authentication rejection still loads the sign-in view. [#161649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161649). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Long-chat local saving reduces repeated serialization and uses idle time when available, with immediate saving on lifecycle changes. [#162004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162004). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Typing, streaming and scrolling through loaded long conversations avoid repeated history processing. [#162035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162035). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large-session views and long chats reduce repeated local run checks and saved-stream selection while keeping Stop available. [#162092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162092). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening the Control UI no longer downloads as much Review display code before it is needed. [#162294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162294). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cold New Session loading downloads less initial JavaScript and makes fewer requests. [#162382](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162382). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cached conversation content, drafts and queues remain usable during connection loss. Prepared Chat and New Session pages can reopen when the browser is explicitly offline, with completed cache and retained access information. Uncertain sends wait for receipt checks and recovery review; cached data remains readable to anyone who can use that browser profile. [#162428](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162428). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat controls and viewer avatars avoid redundant updates while real content, access and presence changes still refresh them. [#162536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162536). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tooltips create their popup on first use and reuse it while keeping keyboard focus and accessible descriptions available. [#162539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162539). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large conversations and lists avoid unnecessary styling work and measure offscreen labels when they first become visible. [#162589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162589). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Switching, scrolling and resizing chats avoid repeated layout work while preserving reading position and end anchoring. [#162617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162617). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Simultaneous clients have more bounded waiting capacity for setup and session requests. Overloaded Gateways can still refuse requests. [#162668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162668). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible cached conversation routes can display before Gateway reconnect finishes. Missing or ambiguous cached routing still waits for live verification. [#162794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162794). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening Approvals for the first time avoids initializing unrelated chat and redaction work. [#162808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162808). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The first stored conversation after startup can load sooner. [#162936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162936). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Hidden retained chat panes pause updates and apply them on return, while visible split panes continue updating. [#163073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163073). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The first browser connection after a restart avoids heavier startup-scheduling preparation. [#163173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163173). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reconnect bursts prioritize connection handshakes and spread retries, which can leave the first session lists taking longer to arrive. [#163714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163714). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Concurrent conversation-history reads prepare more of their results in background processing. [#163771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163771). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resizing chats avoids redundant redraws while retaining scroll anchoring and hidden-pane geometry. [#163898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163898). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large Claude CLI conversations use ordinary bounded pages and backscroll for local and native-only messages. Initial indexing still scans history, and an unavailable native transcript can still block loading despite readable local history. [#164419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164419). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Device-control text loads when Devices needs it, reducing the initial JavaScript download. [#164735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164735). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Gateway can spend less time preparing the Control UI's browser files, particularly when there are many files. [4cf8c4d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4cf8c4d334a18a521f94314b0abbe357ff0bf470). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

**Bug fixes**

- Interrupted provider sign-in closes its reserved blank tab and explains the reconnect or startup timeout. Check again handles a sign-in whose result is no longer known. [#160954](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160954). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- The Applying indicator animates before the settings page finishes loading. [#161102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161102). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Send waits during account recovery while the draft remains editable. It becomes available after account recovery even if ordinary history is still loading, and never sends the held draft automatically. [#161695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161695). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Live conversation updates avoid an unrelated display-refresh wait. Slow subscriptions gain [diagnostic phase logs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/logging). A scoped restoration check lets [Windows update cleanup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) proceed after interruption; it cannot repair an older update already in progress. [#162013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162013). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An eligible web-chat attempt that stalls before answering can make one recovery attempt in the same conversation. Failure leaves retry guidance; personal or workspace skill-write permission requires a fresh user message. [#162490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162490). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- If both the original and recovery attempts stall before output, Please try again returns instead of leaving the conversation stuck. [#162713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162713). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Older open tabs wait for retained UI-file inventory after a Gateway restart. Tabs whose files have been pruned still need to reload. [#163090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163090). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retained UI builds avoid storing compressed duplicates, keeping earlier tabs usable within the existing three-generation and 96 MiB limits. Earlier-build downloads use larger uncompressed files. [#163099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163099). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Review stays unloaded when its tab is absent, while an existing hidden Review tab retains its content. [#163346](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163346). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Paired Codex disconnection keeps guidance to start a fresh attempt. [#163515](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163515). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- An agent still preparing keeps a startup message instead of a settled failure, even after a minute. [#164041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164041). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Normal agent preparation shows Starting up and automatically loads chat and sidebar data when ready. Preparation can take minutes; real failures keep their diagnostics. [#164532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164532). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repaired container bundles let restricted arbitrary user IDs read the core Control UI assets, addressing the whole-UI 503 failure. Install or build the repaired image; existing deployments are not changed automatically, and private runtime files remain restricted. [#165937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165937). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@Conan-Scott](https://github.com/Conan-Scott).
- After a reconnect or profile change, the Control UI preserves the conversation lists and chat-startup information saved by the new connection. [943286f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/943286f39b80e2135c3f57cd74cfa4de5d7f8fc7). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat updates return to on-demand scheduling after removal of the frame wait that could hold working indicators and draft updates. [d1dc3cd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d1dc3cd51104d5592968802de29b54002578117a). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Workspaces, dashboards and terminals">

Review and Files follow a newly prepared checkout, and file links use the sender's workspace. Permitted session-folder HTML previews can load relative assets, including in unsaved drafts. Files outside the workspace need the agent's current authorization and remain read-only previews within account and sandbox restrictions. Dashboard reads follow accepted changes, and embedded MCP Apps fill their panels again in fullscreen, resized and split layouts.

A [progress card](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/progress-card) now has separate hide and clear actions. X hides it only in your pane, including for viewers, and reload or a Gateway connection change restores it. Writers use Clear saved progress for everyone to remove the shared saved card.

Admins can use separate [PR automation controls](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) for repair or comments, merge when ready, and archiving. These jobs run every five minutes, need a running Gateway and scheduler, use model allowance, and can continue after browser close or restart. A browser role change does not cancel an existing job. Canceling prevents later runs but cannot undo an accepted external action; archiving the selected session can also clean its managed worktree.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Split-dashboard widgets use one full-width title, with resize controls in the task menu. [#160117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160117). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Admins can separately schedule PR repair or comments, merge when ready, and archive controls from the CI popup. Jobs run every five minutes, use model allowance and can survive browser close or Gateway restart. Canceling prevents future runs but cannot undo an action already accepted by GitHub. [#161492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161492). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- PR automation controls, status, help and errors use the existing translated locales. [#161688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161688).
- Cmd+K on Apple devices or Ctrl+K elsewhere opens the command palette from a normal-chat dashboard widget. Escape returns to the same input, caret and draft. [#162369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162369). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- The CI popup uses less space while retaining its three automation controls, status, job links and errors. [#162444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162444). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Existing translations cover import results, admin access to folders outside the workspace and catalog-check guidance. [#162451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162451).
- File links follow the sender's workspace. Files outside it need the agent's current authorization and receive read-only previews within sandbox and account restrictions. Session-folder HTML can load permitted relative images, media, styles and scripts, including in unsaved drafts; local fonts, CSS imports and folderless attachments remain outside that support. Relative assets are limited to 64 references, 1 MiB each and 4 MiB per request, with a 256 KiB cloud limit. [#163185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163185). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- PR previews reuse unchanged checkout information while selected-ref, staged, configuration and ancestry changes remain visible on the next read. Unstaged-only counts can stay stale for five minutes, including after refresh. [#164035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164035). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Progress-card X hides the card only in the current pane, including for viewers. Reload or a Gateway connection change restores it. Writers use the separate Clear saved progress for everyone trash action to remove the saved card for peers. [4e529ba](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4e529baa8f6dc431fd93f8591859a022d29858fb). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

**Bug fixes**

- Custom workspace-group defaults show the required admin access and offer Retry after folder inspection fails. Saving still requires permission and a successful inspection. [#139024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139024). Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@hewal-dev](https://github.com/hewal-dev) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- The browser dock and embedded panels share page space without competing layouts. [#157533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157533). Thanks [@sonofakel](https://github.com/sonofakel) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Theme-aware inline widgets can follow dark mode where their widget theme messages support it. [#160940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160940). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Host-information views avoid unauthorized requests and retire stale results. Host statistics stay unavailable when the account lacks access. [#160982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160982). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Accounts with portal read access can open the panel, while previews require write access. Accounts without read access see an access explanation. [#160984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160984). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Focusing an embedded dashboard dismisses Inbox without taking focus or discarding notes. Ordinary window blur leaves it open. [#160997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160997). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Review and Files follow a newly ready checkout during the first run, keeping an existing diff on same-workspace refreshes and showing no changes while preparation is pending. [#161010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161010). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Confirmed non-Git folders hide checkout controls, and switching from cloud or device storage to local does not carry forced isolation. A failed Git check requires a successful retry before creation. [#161475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161475). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Transparent, theme-aware MCP Apps blend with the dashboard in light and dark modes; apps with their own opaque background retain it. [#161494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161494). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Branch statistics include untracked text files whose names start with whitespace. [#161723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161723). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- System busyness expand and minimize animations survive unrelated updates and still respond to real size changes. [#162315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162315). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The browser terminal keeps its pending output and reports the original cleanup error. [#162520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162520). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Local workspace icons load without opening chat and can recover after cache eviction, falling back to a folder icon after bounded retries. [#162885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162885). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Enter on an invalid relative folder path preserves the input and listing while keeping that path unselectable. [#163284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163284). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unused vertical scrolling inside an inline widget passes through to chat. New video guidance can load a preview before Play, so it may download media in advance. [#163762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163762). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Malformed terminal attachments release their listener, slash commands retain their supplied help text, and MCP setup can continue when the browser has supported cryptography but lacks its UUID helper. [#164011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164011). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The terminal stays closed until overlapping shutdown waits have all ended, preventing partial or duplicate releases from reopening it early. [#164014](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164014). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Native Windows managed workspaces can be created or reused from deeply nested source repositories that hit Git's metadata-path-too-big error. Cross-drive metadata and separate deep-reference reconciliation limits remain. [#164249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164249). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- RGB-aware programs in local browser terminals use their intended colors when the host lacks COLORTERM. Explicit environment choices and catalog overrides retain precedence. [#164325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164325). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Dashboard, widget and MCP App reads follow previously accepted board writes, avoiding older widget revisions. A changed destination rejects the read instead of serving from its former location. [#164448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164448). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Direct-linked automation editors and unsaved edits survive delayed initial agent information. Deliberate agent-filter changes and connection retirement keep their existing reset behavior. [a7d093b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a7d093b7e2d0c5e1d58e1e9c7185afbe49985a44). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- MCP Apps fill their dashboard panel again in fullscreen, resized and split layouts instead of shrinking above a blank gap. [11515ce](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/11515ce5b3dad2dde1c8a667c6252f62085dd6de). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Activity and shared conversations">

Participants can [react to saved shared-chat messages](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat#reactions) without waking the agent. Telegram and WhatsApp can restore another reaction after removal, though some removals still leave the wrong channel reaction when several people chose the same emoji. Assignment and membership menus put all loaded matching people and agents in one searchable list. Supported Discord or Slack browser plugins can offer a session-header link back to the first source conversation; Slack and GovSlack links open the channel or DM rather than an exact message or thread.

Chat activity shows the actual tool called, keeps a readable fallback when detail is absent, and stops calling a completed tool Running. When no definite result exists, it shows Outcome unknown. Completed-work headings spell out elapsed time, and delegated work can show Waiting on subagents, handoff and resumption. Eligible Activity updates arrive directly from events; compact rows can still retain some prior-model details after a model change.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- New channel conversations retain an already-known sender name for the sharing Owner field. Older conversations and missing names keep their existing fallback. [#160923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160923). Thanks [@stevenlee-oai](https://github.com/stevenlee-oai).
- A single permitted publishing destination uses a direct button. Choosing a personal account explicitly can request publication; picking an account in a multi-account menu leaves publication for the next action. [#160967](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160967). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Identified participants can react to saved shared-chat prompts and replies without waking the agent. Supported channel source prompts can mirror the reaction as the bot; a failed mirror leaves the local reaction saved. [#161053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161053). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat activity uses tool icons with exact names on hover or through assistive technology, while expansion keeps the details and outcomes available. [#161483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161483). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Open person cards keep up to three already-loaded, accessible recent conversation links with current times and presence. Removed entries disappear until the card is reopened. [#161493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161493). Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Eligible same-origin HTTPS still screenshots can use a smaller preview while opening keeps the original. Animated images and failed or larger conversions keep the original preview. [#161602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161602). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- GitHub hovercards bound their upstream wait and can omit optional pictures or coauthors. Slow required information offers a retryable unavailable result; a partial card may need refresh. [#161653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161653). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reaction labels, hints and participant text use the existing twenty translated locales. [#161785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161785).
- Current PR status is separated from a previous failed publishing attempt. Failure details remain expandable, and pending confirmation stays visible. [#162173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162173). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The previous-failed-attempt label uses the existing translated locales. [#162185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162185).
- Eligible guests can publish a PR from their own session through a shared account and qualified target. Personal publication still requires broader write access. [#162988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162988). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Notification reads and saves move database preparation into background processing while retaining access checks and subscription order. [#163355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163355). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delegated-work rows show the task and plain named child titles, with Subagent as the unnamed fallback. [#163745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163745). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- A selected conversation shows Waiting on subagents when only its descendants are active, with a child link when exactly one active direct child is identifiable. Successful handoff and later resumption remain visible when tool details are hidden. [#164190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164190). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed-work headings spell out elapsed time, with call totals on expansion and non-success outcomes visible while collapsed. Missing timing leaves Worked without an invented duration. [#164430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164430). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Assignment and membership menus show all already-loaded matching people and agents in one scrollable list with search kept visible. [#164469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164469). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible Activity and Current Work changes appear directly from session events, with smaller sidebar and dashboard list responses. Filtered or incomplete views still refresh, and a model change can leave some compact-row model details from the previous model. [#164573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164573). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Supported Discord and Slack browser plugins can link the session header back to its first source conversation. Slack and GovSlack open the channel or DM rather than an exact message or thread; older sessions acquire a link from a later inbound event. [a49a332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a49a3321fa9a4f15d50246ccd2daf456acbbaa48). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

**Bug fixes**

- Activity screenshot strips use downloadable images, retain their layout during refresh and show an unavailable tile when a remote image fails. Large images require a reachable download reference. [#161021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161021). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Activity totals use the complete matching time range, including sessions beyond the displayed list. The all-time chart shows the latest twelve months of activity. [#161060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161060). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Enter on a hovered Assign to control keeps the person menu open instead of assigning the session to yourself. [#161393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161393). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Screenshot-only recap updates no longer leave a misleading omitted-messages notice. Genuine omitted messages still show the notice. [#161518](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161518). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Assignment failures display the Gateway error, and the current owner is checked and disabled in the picker. [#161562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161562). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Returning to Live activity restores the active tool filter, including after an older unselected tool disappears. [#161860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161860). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A shared-publication warning retires when GitHub proves the same accepted work was already published. Newer, unverified or unavailable results keep the recovery warning. [#162257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162257). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Durable reactions keep local changes even when a changed channel route prevents mirroring. Telegram and WhatsApp removals can restore another reaction, with a remaining limitation when several people chose the same emoji. [#162434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162434). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex reasoning and compaction activity no longer appear as empty completed tool cards. Genuine tool activity remains visible. [#162758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162758). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@DarkJuanFra](https://github.com/DarkJuanFra) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- GitHub icons stay beside the first line of wrapped labels in narrow and right-to-left layouts. [#163047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163047). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A failed Mention Inbox refresh retries after a minute instead of waiting for a distant expiry. [#163229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163229). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tool cards distinguish unfinished work from reported success, failure or interruption, including recognized Wait and matching agent-completion events. An unknown outcome stays unknown. [#163545](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163545). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Tool Search activity cards show the tool actually called and its query, file or command instead of only the dispatcher. [#163829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163829). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session and Workboard summaries accept valid plain or JSON-fenced observer replies while rejecting malformed content. [#163878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163878). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tool activity keeps a readable label such as Wait or Session Status when there is no distinct detail to show. [#164437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164437). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat-scoped connections avoid unauthorized PR and Discussion reads, clear stale badges after access loss and retain permitted shared-publication options. [bf2883a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bf2883a22e341cdc0cbbdd60fadfacf505b68c62). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Completed tools stop showing Running while the assistant continues. Without a definite result they show Outcome unknown; partial output alone keeps the tool running. [7b3972c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7b3972c677e20444376aeac1f3b2860633467b9d). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Settings and plugin discovery">

Identity editing gains a searchable emoji picker, Quiet Hours uses a timezone menu, and long model names wrap inside the picker. Eligible sessions can select Ultrafast, with availability determined by the account, model and runtime. New credential fields accept the full masked value, while stored redacted secrets and structured references remain protected. The [permission menu](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/permission-modes) also separates its heading from the help link.

Plugin discovery reuses the Featured and Trending shelves after a settled search is cleared. Cards retain available artwork while status loads, and details can expand published MCP setup, authentication and permissions information. Those details describe setup rather than live login or connection status. Rapid category and type changes followed by clearing can still display the previous category.
