</AccordionGroup>

## Updates and Maintenance

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Backups on external storage">

Keep full backups on an external drive, mounted storage or Cloudflare R2, check the destination from OpenClaw, and restore into a fresh staging directory before deciding what to activate. Configuring a target does not schedule backups by itself. You can enable a Gateway schedule, which defaults to every 24 hours, and optional retention that removes eligible older archives only after a successful backup while keeping the newest one.

Choose a passphrase for encrypted uploads, or explicitly choose `none` for plaintext. Destination names and markers remain visible, and `--output` keeps a plaintext local archive even when the uploaded copy is encrypted. Keep the passphrase, destination marker and namespace for recovery. Give simultaneously running clones separate namespaces; take over an existing namespace only after its previous owner stops. Full restore checks the archive’s logical size plus a 256 MiB reserve when free space can be measured.

See [backup destinations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/storage), [storage locations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/storage-locations), [Cloudflare R2](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/cloudflare) and the [backup and restore guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/backups).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Full backups support external storage and Cloudflare R2, optional schedules and retention, destination checks and restoration into fresh staging. [#161913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161913). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Full restore checks archive size plus a 256 MiB free-space reserve before extraction when destination capacity can be measured. [#164492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164492). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@smoe](https://github.com/smoe).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve settings and recovery copies">

Updates recheck settings saved during supported validation steps, including included files, so compatible edits can be retained. A changed stored channel triggers fresh target selection; an explicit channel or source still controls the request. Candidate-incompatible settings stop activation, and repeated edits can exhaust validation time. Separate caller and service profiles keep their own compatibility plans.

Doctor and direct updates attempt to retain original state before repairs, with clearer notices when capture is incomplete. These captures are manual recovery material. Keep a verified full backup for complete or lasting recovery, and preserve current data before restoring an older capture. Any database changes made during Doctor disable automatic restoration of that database. Restore configuration, shared and agent databases, and credentials from one matching backup generation, with room for the extracted copy.

Webhook completion alone no longer rewrites configuration at startup. Actual webhook repair requires `openclaw doctor --fix` and a restart; externally managed read-only config needs the indicated source edits. Update rehearsals can preserve historical listeners for Telegram, Feishu, Teams and Nextcloud Talk when a missing official plugin install is deferred, without running unrelated plugin repairs.

The [original-state recovery guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#original-state-captures) explains retained captures; [rollback and recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery) covers matched backups and restoration.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Failed-update recovery preserves databases changed during Doctor, including legitimate migrations; restoring those databases then requires manual recovery. [#160167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160167). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Repair preserves settings for incompatible plugins and finishes pending saved-data confirmation after compatibility is corrected, including when packages are unchanged. [#160344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160344). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@EndeavorPioneer](https://github.com/EndeavorPioneer).
- Eligible database rollback retains current update attempts and failure details while leaving the original snapshots unchanged. [#160614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160614). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Direct updates and standalone Doctor repairs attempt to retain original state before changing it, with clearer incomplete-capture status; complete recovery still needs a verified backup. [#161044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161044). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Interrupted update validation waits for accepted progress writes and retains temporary recovery data when their outcome is uncertain. [#161104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161104). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unresolved original-state captures now point to the retained manifest and manual recovery guide, with instructions to preserve current data before restoring anything. [#161292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161292). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor reduces pre-repair capture overhead and, after a successful fresh capture, removes eligible completed standalone recovery copies older than 30 days; use a verified backup for lasting recovery. [#162364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162364). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Database backups captured by a fixed installed updater retain their originally verified content facts so rollback can reject later changes before replacing live databases. [#162383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162383). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recovery copies can share unchanged filesystem pages without sharing later edits; ordinary-copy fallback and free-space requirements remain. [#162466](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162466). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Updates with matching recovery receipts can restore the previous package after SQLite moves already-captured data, while still refusing restoration that would discard newer changes; older updater receipt formats remain a limitation. [#162599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162599). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Rollback prepares restore copies before moving current databases, so allocation or disk-space refusal leaves current databases and journals in place; later publication failures retain recovery files. [#162726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162726). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- On POSIX, an updater containing this fix settles its retained local database writer before capture, avoiding a false rollback refusal after Doctor errors while still protecting genuine newer data. [#163008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163008). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor continues authorized database repairs when an older updater cannot supply capture history, without inventing recovery copies or extending package rollback. [#163344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163344). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor captures relevant Wiki and Teams migration files; blocked Teams archival keeps the token source and reports a warning. [#163780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163780). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor stops before repairs when an explicitly supplied original-state capture cannot be verified; inspect retained update evidence before retrying. [#163808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163808). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can repair temporary update rehearsal databases under verified maintenance ownership while preserving protection for stores outside the rehearsal. [#163869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163869). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore guides explain extra extraction space and recovering configuration, databases and credentials from one matching backup generation. [#164491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164491). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Updates proceed around retained agent-deletion holds while Doctor preserves uncertain stores and explains reserved-agent recovery. [#164509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164509). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@rosmiroslav-create](https://github.com/rosmiroslav-create), [@PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13).
- Updates recheck settings saved during validation and reselect an implicit channel when needed; incompatible or repeatedly changing settings still stop activation. [b36eb3e7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b36eb3e7b11afb2774d1f6ab41977df4713269ff). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway startup leaves webhook config bytes unchanged for completion-only markers; actual repairs require Doctor and a subsequent restart. [b978fc0e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b978fc0e0e7107b1a6bfa00989557e0aa51d2641). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Already-current updates can apply recognized legacy configuration repairs without a channel option, preserving the stored channel. [d1006ac0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d1006ac0a2066bfc78334b4a5433c1ded40a9051). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Candidate configuration checks retain supported legacy repair plans without switching channels or writing the original file. [107b2353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/107b2353d13e2a793ef1fbac6301585b310bccac). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Update rehearsals preserve historical webhook listener settings for Telegram, Feishu, Teams and Nextcloud Talk when a missing official plugin install is deferred. [237b7b91](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/237b7b91fd69e5d3963d9e94eb8f727768530726). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Package updates and recovery">

Update repair can retire untouched preparation without installing its candidate, or verify and settle a candidate already installed by an interrupted update. Verified completion stops obsolete recovery history from blocking the next update, even when later evidence archival produces a warning. Remaining backups and helpers are kept. Changed installed files or launchers, live updaters and unfinished rollback still block settlement. Installation checks verify packaged content and launchers; targets outside `dist` are checked for resolution rather than full content.

For eligible abandoned foreground handoffs, run repair with the failed update’s exact profile and state overrides, preserve its lease database and artifacts, and wait at least 45 minutes from recorded activity—or longer if its recorded timeout requires it. Live or unverifiable processes and incomplete inspection still cause refusal; Windows inspection may need elevation under the same account.

Several Windows, macOS, npm and working-directory fixes belong to the installed updater that starts the operation. If an older updater refuses before staging, manually install a release containing the fix at the same package root, preserving a verified backup and following the managed-Gateway stop instructions, then use the new CLI’s `doctor --fix` and `update repair`. Stop discovered sibling Gateways yourself before replacing their shared installation. A stopped service stays stopped unless the updater owns its restoration. Windows rollback capture now settles pending log writes first; another connection can still limit the snapshot to manual recovery.

Progress stays visible through final reporting, JSON output stays clean, and shareable failure reports retain useful reasons after private details are removed. A served target can make an old failure historical without changing the original result. Eligible completed Linux receipts can refresh device numbers after a remount; the initial `update --dry-run` path can also rewrite those receipts.

**Immutable Linux installations:** explicitly adopt a supported sealed systemd installation after stopping its previous updater. Ordinary adoption and `--no-restart` prepare candidates without switching the serving generation. `adopt-immutable --enable-activation` adds an opt-in prepare, switch, start and verify path, plus guarded recovery. It requires root, the documented systemd service and stable launcher, an adopted external Node runtime outside the Gateway’s cgroup, cgroup v2, matching database schemas and a healthy bridge release whose running Gateway supports suspension handoff. Enable activation only after the former updater has stopped scheduling. Inconclusive startup remains pending. The foundational detector can misidentify an ordinary checkout containing both `current` and `releases`; use the documented immutable layout. This path does not rewind schemas or prune generations.

See [update repair](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery), [manual installation methods](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods#alternative-manual-npm-pnpm-or-bun), the [Bun update guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun) and [immutable release installations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update#immutable-release-installations).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Updates refuse to replace a shared installation while an observed sibling Gateway is running; stop that service yourself and restart it after the update. [#160663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160663). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows Gateway inspection and update repair preserve empty arguments, quotes, backslashes and Unicode when identifying running processes. [#160680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160680). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Incompatible automatic updates from Windows 2026.9.4 stop before activation; wait for the updater to exit, create a verified backup and follow the manual update procedure. [#160718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160718). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The current automatic source-server update script checks a private candidate before stopping the Gateway; preparation failures preserve the serving installation, while shared sibling services remain yours to stop. [#161110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161110). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates and source rebuilds identify discovered sibling Gateways by their actual service name, including loaded Linux services with missing unit files, and explain which service to stop. [#161238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161238). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Source update completion and update repair refuse to replace files used by a discovered running sibling Gateway; stop overlapping services yourself and retry. [#161239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161239). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Older updaters no longer falsely report a changed database inventory and roll back because optional ownership details were omitted. [#161411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161411). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed update recovery skips waiting for a Gateway known to be absent, while retaining the original failure and restoration checks. [#161426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161426). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Source installations retain the previous Control UI bundle when a rebuild fails validation, and a later build can recover an interrupted replacement. [#161438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161438). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates record and check the previous Gateway’s stop in background database workers while still waiting for durable confirmation before maintenance proceeds. [#161813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161813). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed updates record activation before stopping the Gateway and refuse a stop after ownership changes; accepted rollback can finish during interruption. [#161851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161851). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Update recovery loads fewer bundled components while keeping its existing service and recovery checks. [#161919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161919). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows updates run by the fixed installed version restore the original task autostart state after cancellation following successful suspension and before installation changes. [#161990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161990). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows update cancellation can restore previously enabled autostart when disabling the task took effect before reporting failure, provided the original updater still owns recovery. [#162050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162050). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates started by the fixed installed updater retain SQLite verification workers after package replacement, avoiding the missing-worker startup failure. [#162076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162076). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint), [@paweldrozd](https://github.com/paweldrozd).
- Updates wait for saved progress before advancing, preserve authorized recovery after recording refusal and retain both operation and reporting errors. [#162098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162098). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows updates run by the fixed installed updater retry brief package-folder locks during backup for up to 57.75 seconds, retain warnings and explain persistent failures. [#162231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162231). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@miguelpt2026](https://github.com/miguelpt2026).
- Fixed npm updaters exclude historical package backups from dependency scans and retire only eligible captured backups after verified activation; older running updaters still need the documented backup-preserving recovery. [#162246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162246). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Update database checks retry transient changes when an inactive agent database becomes active, returning only a consistent snapshot; persistent changes and cleanup failures still stop inspection. [#162258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162258). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Successful update and repair progress is logged as information, advisories as warnings and failed phases as errors, while progress remains on stderr and JSON stdout stays clean. [#162270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162270). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Failed updates with --no-restart retain recovery diagnostics through one inspection without waiting for unrequested startup, while package activation and required maintenance remain in effect. [#162327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162327). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@PennyVibe](https://github.com/PennyVibe).
- Startup progress no longer hides actual errors from older updaters; reports retain useful redacted error details. [#162344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162344). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jamiezigelbaum](https://github.com/jamiezigelbaum).
- Updates started by the fixed installed updater avoid repeated whole-database copying and stalls from progress polling while retaining committed phases and the final report. [#162345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162345). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint).
- Fixed installed updaters tolerate narrowly defined npm bookkeeping changes during package rollback and report bounded details about genuine mismatches; older running updaters retain their prior checks. [#162365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162365). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nessim-liamani](https://github.com/nessim-liamani).
- Source builds skip redundant plugin file checks and preserve tracked special filenames on supporting filesystems; add new auto-discovered packages and entries to the Git index before a root build. [#162384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162384). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- npm plugin updates reuse metadata already resolved for the same installation attempt, avoiding a duplicate registry lookup while retaining compatibility, integrity, policy and consent checks. [#162385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162385). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates using the repaired verifier warn about non-object plugin manifests and continue checking later plugins, while other configuration, ownership and readiness failures retain their existing handling. [#162386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162386). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Git update staging can use native directory copying when no disposable caches need filtering, avoiding unnecessary staging work while retaining real exclusions. [#162387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162387). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Newer updaters can avoid recopying a private rehearsal database and preserve its original row IDs for migration checks; existing free-space requirements and older-updater limits remain. [#162403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162403). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Update rehearsals avoid redundant backups of verified disposable core database copies while retaining ordinary recovery backups and separate configuration, plugin and Workshop captures. [#162429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162429). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Supported POSIX npm updates can recover interrupted package replacement with the selected Node or Bun executable, retaining the macOS SQLite library; recovery leaves the Gateway stopped for a separate restart. [#162586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162586). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An installed updater containing this fix keeps progress recording from disrupting its own validation copy and reports progress errors without relabeling successful checks as failed. [#162604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162604). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Bun updates from the supported 2026.9.7 updater can finish candidate Doctor and activate the replacement Gateway instead of hanging; this repair arrives in the candidate package. [#162655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162655). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update timeout reports retain the check name, elapsed time and last sanitized progress; old installed updater deadlines still apply and observed progress does not prove completion. [#162883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162883). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nachoratico-source](https://github.com/nachoratico-source).
- Failed update reports put plain runtime-check guidance and the selected next step first, and label saved recovery observations so they are not mistaken for current Gateway health. [#162887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162887). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- When updates hand finalization to a fresh candidate after schema migration, transferred progress is confirmed in order before finalization; a refused write stops further work. [#162909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162909). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An updater containing this change reduces repeated filesystem scans while preserving its runtime, with fallback progress inspection retained when copying stalls; older installed updaters do not gain this on their first attempt. [#162995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162995). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Update snapshots avoid redundant intermediate integrity scans while retaining source and final checks; the published 2026.9.7 updater’s initial snapshot still uses its older implementation. [#162996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162996). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Selected source builds avoid generating pure plugin assets twice while keeping manifest-writing hooks early and validating completed assets before staging. [#162997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162997). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Temporary update-validation Gateways skip restoring retained subagent runs, leaving recovery to the serving Gateway while retaining required configuration, database and readiness checks. [#162998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162998). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An updater containing this change verifies package contents with bounded concurrent reads while preserving fingerprints and fresh recovery checks; the benefit applies to verification work rather than a guaranteed whole-update speedup. [#162999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162999). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- The Bun guide explains the installed 2026.9.7 updater’s temporary Node 24 workaround, keeping Gateway and updater on the same runtime and state, and distinguishing restored package files from completed Gateway recovery. [#163017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163017). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The 2026.9.7 Bun workaround now uses the ordinary published-release update by default; a particular version or local package remains optional, with the same readiness and state-preservation steps. [#163034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163034). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A repaired updater retries eligible named npm or Bun dependency-cache failures once and preserves safe phase, error-code and package details in reports without exposing commands, credentials or private paths. [#163039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163039). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ttcmourao](https://github.com/ttcmourao), [@akrosliving](https://github.com/akrosliving).
- Bun-driven Git updates use compatible Node from PATH consistently for candidate tooling while retaining the chosen Gateway runtime. [#163227](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163227). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Source updates preserve the selected package-manager launcher when the qualified Node directory also contains pnpm. [#163310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163310). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat update notices show a short outcome and next step, with full diagnostics in Settings → Updates or openclaw update status. [#163318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163318). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Updates repeat fewer package scans; completed runtime copies can remain when their layout prevents later cleanup discovery. [#163370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163370). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Git updates keep scoped pnpm ahead of competing copies even when the selected Node directory already leads or repeats on PATH. [#163380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163380). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Later npm updates reuse settled file checks to shorten downtime; stop other tools that modify the installation while updating. [#163432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163432). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Routine package-backup cleanup is logged as progress instead of a misleading update error. [#163477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163477). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- npm upgrades retain installation identity through macOS directory aliases while the package directory is temporarily absent. [#163479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163479). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Startup Git discovery retries one timeout and reports unavailable facts; exhausted results stay cached, with Dev refresh or restart needed to recover. [#163500](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163500). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unrelated ancestor packages stop falsely blocking update checks, and two matching candidate-Doctor failures pause automatic retries until a new candidate or manual intervention. [#163541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163541). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@iJaack](https://github.com/iJaack).
- Git update and rollback reports show both recorded version numbers and short commits. [#163683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163683). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Eligible update recovery checks and completes pending shared and agent migrations before restarting; unresolved or newer schemas refuse with manual guidance. [#163803](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163803). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@chffhc](https://github.com/chffhc).
- Failed updates retain their specific reason and original installer error on stderr in normal and JSON modes. [#163901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163901). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Status marks a saved update failure as historical when the ready local Gateway serves the intended target, preserving the original failure record. [#163991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163991). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sergiorodriguez-sudo](https://github.com/sergiorodriguez-sudo).
- Future npm updates using the fixed installed updater verify fresh package contents with bounded hashing workers and unchanged integrity checks. [#164052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164052). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updated nodes accept the Skill Workshop launch field used by a 2026.9.8 Gateway, preserving affected hosted turns. [#164068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164068). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update reports retain recognized worker error causes while removing private paths and filename suffixes. [#164169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164169). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixed installed updaters avoid discarded duplicate configuration reads while retaining fresh edits and database compatibility checks. [#164182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164182). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin snapshots and runtime preparation overlap bounded metadata checks without changing discovery order or integrity checks. [#164183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164183). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Verified pnpm 11 and 12 isolated updates skip an unnecessary global-root subprocess while retaining fallback discovery. [#164184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164184). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Trusted official plugin prerelease updates reuse successful package-selection metadata within the same attempt, retaining integrity, consent and warnings. [#164185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164185). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Caller and service profiles using the exact same state path share one database backup per checkpoint, with fresh checks retained. [#164186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164186). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed-update advice correctly identifies a verified serving Gateway while preserving failed status and restart or rollback restrictions. [#164193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164193). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Supported Linux immutable installations add explicitly enabled generation switching and recovery with documented root, service, launcher and schema requirements. [#164306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164306). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update JSON output omits the human cleanup-progress line while retaining structured cleanup results. [#164312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164312). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repair timeout JSON retains the failed result and original stuck phase even when Gateway restoration also fails. [#164337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164337). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixed updaters accept recreated command links with unchanged targets; older Claude Code recovery prompts warn that normal customization applies. [#164438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164438). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Non-root macOS updates tolerate launcher ownership they cannot reproduce; stranded operations have a guarded recovery path after manual installation of a fixed CLI at the same root. [#164636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164636). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hrp6vv9mwr-glitch](https://github.com/hrp6vv9mwr-glitch), [@cmillwat](https://github.com/cmillwat).
- A fixed CLI can repair a package update blocked by replacement of its ownership database while retaining staged files, the helper and recovery evidence. [#164849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164849). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@szef-svg](https://github.com/szef-svg), [@izrl613](https://github.com/izrl613).
- Updates from 2026.10.1-beta.1 retain the cleanup imports needed after package replacement. [#165855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165855). Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- The new updater refreshes eligible completed Linux receipts after device-number changes; initial update dry-run can also rewrite those receipts. [#166394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166394). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@neilofneils404](https://github.com/neilofneils404).
- The 2026.10.1-beta.2 updater bridge inventory is restored and excludes standalone worker copies from bridge selection. [#167091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167091). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@adele-with-a-b](https://github.com/adele-with-a-b), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Candidate compatibility checks leave original profile databases and their SQLite sidecar files unchanged when preservation is requested. [5a19f360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5a19f3608c28baf0a890526c82bf4e09f5dde32b). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Candidate checks preserve original agent database files when requested and still refuse newer schemas saved only in the log. [89217d3b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/89217d3b875373f5ba4f63dfc5e23fa9ca744ba6). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explicit channel changes keep caller and managed-service compatibility checks separate, preserving each profile’s settings. [1794d8b4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1794d8b4ef8dde46f39a16da2bdbcf0bf2b519ef). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update progress saves run in database workers; delayed saves cannot let a replaced or revoked update continue. [ea4aaef1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ea4aaef16715c65bf9a9530a1e275bdbf84f548e). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update verification can continue when its starting directory disappears by launching the read-only worker from a valid temporary directory. [8e303782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8e303782bb5677edbb27c77c222b568fb692dbbf). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Corrected installed updaters preserve relative user, session and local-package paths while replacing an installation that contains their starting directory. [6830c82e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6830c82ea080767b8953edce13a3e543eed419da). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explicit update repair can reclaim eligible abandoned handoffs after the required grace period, with retained evidence, dead-process checks and unfinished-rollback refusals. [9680e57a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9680e57a9631b6a7060b9d4642bffb9f9fbc3000). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@taihartman](https://github.com/taihartman).
- Interactive update progress remains visible through final verification and result reporting. [82fe5e96](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/82fe5e962f72a54bc80c43cb9b2a116228bafb3b). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible Windows updates settle pending database log writes before rollback capture; another connection still limits snapshots to manual recovery. [9863e3f9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9863e3f97ab4ad345ebbdcb575e6d310b13d40a1). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Blank migration-plan snapshot paths receive the specific argument error in both human and machine output. [71b857a7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/71b857a75b52b64bded377bda4f53086897a8283). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explicit adoption prepares sealed Linux candidates away from the serving generation, with later activation requiring a separate opt-in. [99babf27](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/99babf27254314921f7e71a8c8b710b10a851ad8). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shareable failed-update reports retain a public-safe reason after private details are removed, including failures before any command ran. [fc7e71ba](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fc7e71bad25845ba8c23ddd6f3c44ded11a9f427). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A fixed installed CLI can retire unused preparation or verify an already installed interrupted candidate, clearing obsolete recovery gates while preserving evidence and unfinished-restoration checks. [5a5966fa](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5a5966fa994f637c624f0ec493cf29893bced148). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Doctor and database repair">

Doctor can begin repair of quarantined state by inspecting a private snapshot, while still refusing an active or unverifiable Gateway owner. Supported older Claw ownership-history columns receive a backup before repair; affected planning and resume previews first ask for `openclaw doctor --fix`. Package removal keeps protections for shared packages and operator-modified files, and an advisory inspection failure no longer misreports a completed plugin uninstall.

Repairs use fewer repeated scans and copies, preserve accepted settings when an individual plugin repair fails, and finish accepted database cleanup before releasing maintenance resources. Deep session-owner chains can be repaired without losing conversation history. A broken installed repair module reports its original error. Explicitly supplied original-state captures must verify before changes begin.

When repair finishes but the managed Gateway is still starting, Doctor warns and can exit successfully. Check `openclaw gateway status` afterward to confirm readiness. A retained `Doctor complete.` message records check completion; a later crash or failing exit still fails update validation.

For an externally managed read-only configuration, Doctor can repair writable state and report the required external edits. Review those edits before returning to an older image: its state may require a matched backup. See [Doctor migrations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations), [running Doctor](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/running) and [SQLite maintenance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Doctor checks that an explicitly selected session store belongs to the requested agent; scripts with mismatched selectors must use its actual owner. [#156907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156907). Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Doctor accepts a service home override pointing to the account’s canonical home, restoring the existing credential-repair path. [#160056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160056). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@oscarsixsecllc](https://github.com/oscarsixsecllc), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- An already-stopped Gateway stays stopped when Doctor cannot verify its current ownership and offline state; inspect deep status before starting it manually. [#160083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160083). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and plugin maintenance can continue through brief competing database writes without immediately failing admission or losing maintenance ownership. [#160702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160702). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@Ludwigtheo0815](https://github.com/Ludwigtheo0815), [@laurentschwartz](https://github.com/laurentschwartz).
- Unmanaged foreground Gateways can remain alive for manual configuration repair after restart triage declines or fails; requests resume only after startup succeeds. [#161294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161294). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js).
- Offline Doctor btrfs repair no longer falsely refuses shared and agent stores because of ownership checks or a newly created empty database log; the Gateway must remain stopped. [#161501](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161501). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor clears eligible installation-only upgrade warnings for disabled SMS and unconfigured Mistral without enabling the channel or adding settings; real migration work still needs completion. [#161535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161535). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@akennedytog](https://github.com/akennedytog), [@g62nkcx4q7-wq](https://github.com/g62nkcx4q7-wq).
- Doctor recognizes verified Skill Workshop history archives after their original workspace is retired, stopping repeated warnings while retaining original backups. [#161586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161586). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@percilabs](https://github.com/percilabs), [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng).
- Doctor refuses to overwrite a recovery record after another owner replaces its maintenance files, retaining the existing recovery information. [#161861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161861). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and local TLS Gateway checks work with a managed proxy enabled while retaining loopback routing policy and certificate fingerprint checks. [#161946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161946). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@saizero-dr](https://github.com/saizero-dr).
- Doctor moves its initial contended Gateway ownership inspection into a background worker and checks cancellation and permission again before stopping a service. [#162021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162021). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat-requested updates avoid repeated whole-database copies while Doctor checks current permissions; removing or replacing the requester still stops further changes. [#162200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162200). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DasX](https://github.com/DasX), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint).
- Doctor skips loading Codex recovery helpers when there is no legacy assignment state to inspect, reducing unnecessary work in empty scans. [#162215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162215). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor reuses its update-history discovery snapshot to reduce repeated reads and offers optional phase timings for diagnosing slow repairs. [#162232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162232). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor repairs deep chains of session owners without a stack overflow, preserving the terminal session and conversation history without shortening the chain. [#162321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162321). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jasdeepohri-max](https://github.com/jasdeepohri-max).
- Doctor uses less memory preparing large fleets with equivalent plugin sources, while preserving distinct plugin selections and workspaces. [#162394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162394). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
- Doctor prepares unchanged transcript archives before stopping a managed Gateway and reuses verified work on later same-version runs, reducing archive-dependent downtime while rechecking changed or restored sources. [#162404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162404). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@MikyWang](https://github.com/MikyWang), [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415).
- Doctor skips unused plugin completion checks when there is no deferred migration or verified retained legacy session source, avoiding an unnecessary detector pass while retaining ordinary repair checks. [#162486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162486). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves previously accepted settings when a plugin repair fails or reports no changes, continues later repairs and retains warning-only guidance. [#162641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162641). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A present but broken channel repair module now stops Doctor with its original load error instead of silently selecting fallback repairs; genuinely absent modules remain optional. [#162645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162645). Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Doctor retires verified completed plugin-import obligations so later history can be imported, and update cleanup inventories recorded migration backups with guarded retirement after a later successful update; retain backups needed for rollback. [#162771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162771). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@shadow-enthusiast](https://github.com/shadow-enthusiast), [@islandpreneur007](https://github.com/islandpreneur007).
- Managed updates defer optional btrfs NOCOW database rewrites by default, leaving directories in place while retaining advice; explicit Doctor repair outside managed updates keeps its existing behavior. [#162786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162786). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Offline btrfs repair refusals now distinguish process holders from failed inspection and show useful process IDs or errors while leaving the original store in place. [#162809](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162809). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can remove eligible abandoned updater runtimes after its inspection workers finish, identifies independent processes that still block cleanup and advises rerunning repair after their work ends. [#162886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162886). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor releases its own authentication readers and checks large btrfs stores in bounded batches before offline NOCOW repair; external holders or inspection failures still block replacement. [#162919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162919). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor recovers supported empty transcripts from verified backups or canonical history. With shared owners, recovery may move an empty original before every agent verifies it, leaving another agent's recovery record unable to find the file; keep originals and backups together at their reported paths. [#162946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162946). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@leoreq](https://github.com/leoreq).
- Optional offline btrfs NOCOW repair preserves ordinary symbolic links and empty files without following link targets; unsupported file types and database aliases remain refused. [#163023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163023). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates prepare eligible older databases to reclaim deleted pages, with one-time downtime and temporary-space costs; uncertain conversions still fail safely. [#163105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163105). Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Doctor correctly names the installed plugin index in its existing manifest-drift warning. [#163220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163220). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor archive maintenance avoids repeated full-database hashing and copying while checking current repair permissions. [#163369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163369). Thanks [@victor-gurbani](https://github.com/victor-gurbani), [@Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Legacy memory conversion processes vectors one row at a time, and updated updaters retain crash signals and bounded redacted diagnostics. [#163640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163640). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fenglanhua](https://github.com/fenglanhua).
- Doctor recognizes verified Linux systemd environment overrides and still reports genuinely missing managed settings. [#163847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163847). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@travellingsoldier85](https://github.com/travellingsoldier85).
- Verified session-import receipts survive macOS VM device-number changes; Doctor upgrades older receipts without replaying completed imports. [#163904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163904). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@paulotvlincelia](https://github.com/paulotvlincelia).
- Migration planning explains an invalid copied-state path before inspecting delivery queues. [#164007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164007). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Database opening and index repair avoid repeated metadata checks while retaining full integrity and foreign-key safeguards. [#164099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164099). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor media repair removes redundant table scans and still refuses concurrent changes; stop competing database users before retrying. [#164139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164139). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Supported service recovery and local diagnostics remain usable with invalid configuration while startup validation and newer-version guards remain. [#164158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164158). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor repairs writable state under externally managed read-only config and reports required source edits; older-image rollback may need a matched backup. [#164235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164235). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@JD8855122](https://github.com/JD8855122).
- Completed Doctor repairs warn and exit successfully when the managed Gateway is still starting; check gateway status afterward to confirm readiness. [#164261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164261). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault).
- Doctor can publish unchanged pre-migration backups when Linux creation-time metadata is unavailable, retaining file identity, size, hash and ownership checks. [#164363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164363). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@StevenKrebs](https://github.com/StevenKrebs), [@kirylh](https://github.com/kirylh), [@stunt4711](https://github.com/stunt4711).
- Doctor finishes database cleanup before releasing maintenance resources, preventing its leftover leases from blocking a later repair. [#164503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164503). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Doctor reports the actual newer-database or recovery-manifest result; failed deletion of a disposable inspection snapshot warns after its reader closes. [#164697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164697). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reports retain Doctor’s check-completion message after a later crash; a nonzero or signal exit still fails validation. [#164853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164853). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@cunzai007-hub](https://github.com/cunzai007-hub).
- Doctor imports legacy pending replies from JSON sessions without repeating the repair refusal, preserving exact session IDs, transcripts and original archive bytes. [#165866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165866). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
- Doctor checks the SQLite directory again after checking open files, refusing unexpected new files before NOCOW replacement. [4d1890a3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4d1890a3cdc3b8f31ac70bb63e19723fee0cc6a9). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor backs up and repairs supported Claw ownership-history columns before planning; package removal retains current ownership and protects modified or shared files. [de1f4385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/de1f4385dc66f31f586b79f3e274007e0e3da253). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Snapshot errors keep their original explanation when temporary-file cleanup also fails. [20f485c7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/20f485c7af9185eb29707fb7d8d879ce6b88ca33). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can inspect quarantined state to begin repair when no active or unverifiable Gateway owner blocks maintenance; ordinary reads still respect quarantine. [6d4ee0af](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6d4ee0afd92e9636b81b6e9bdb65a7367f072bc6). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Upgrade older configuration and state">

Older formats now stop before unsupported conversion and retain their original files, with guidance for the intermediate version that can repair them. Back up the complete configuration and state—including registration files, transcripts and queue-owned media—before taking that step.

Use **2026.9.5 Doctor** for the documented retired sandbox, embedded-agent, Browser, webchat, authored plugin-install, Telegram, Talk and model-timeout forms. Matrix’s five retired state files need 2026.9.5 Doctor and starting Matrix once; older flat layouts first need 2026.6 Doctor and Gateway startup. Use **2026.9.7 on the original host, then `openclaw doctor --fix`**, for retired outgoing/session JSON queues, cron files, encrypted OAuth sidecars, Voice Call logs and nested workspace setup files. Do not rename `.migrated` files or `.media.migrated` directories into queues to replay messages. Supported SQLite queues keep their existing retries and gain no new expiry cutoff. Candidate plugin-index checks can name the 9.7 bridge, while direct Doctor’s plugin-index guidance still uses its documented 9.5 route.

Experimental session layouts require a complete-copy recovery through 2026.9.7 Doctor; a newer database may instead need its pre-update backup. Obsolete memory indexes retain their files and tables rather than being dropped. Some retired session settings and shortcuts need backed-up manual correction instead of automatic conversion. Supported July session-routing aliases, supported JSON imports and current SQLite state remain separately supported.

See [state migrations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations), [session-setting migrations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#session-settings), [retired Matrix state files](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/matrix-migration#retired-pre-july-state-files) and [incoming-queue migration](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate#migrate-durable-ingress-files-through-doctor).

<Accordion title="Storage, restarts and runtime diagnostics">

A crash-loop pause keeps interrupted turns and retries intact, then rearms replay after a full stable window. Another crash extends the pause; shutdown or replacement cancels the pending rearm. This changes replay scheduling without fixing the underlying native crash or deciding that every old completed turn should resume. Configuration restarts also survive duplicate file-watcher notifications, and live access-policy changes retain running or queued work only while its original access stays valid.

Container ownership checks use physical process identity and recent heartbeats across PID namespaces. Verified live owners continue to block maintenance; a foreign or unverifiable owner needs a heartbeat older than 90 seconds before eligible recovery. Doctor refuses a fresh unknown owner immediately. For a separate Docker CLI container, use the documented shared namespace or run the TUI inside the Gateway container. On Windows, Doctor and `gateway start` can re-enable a verified disabled task without taking over a foreign launcher.

Optional offline btrfs NOCOW repair can convert eligible databases for in-place writes, with better holder diagnostics and a fresh file inventory before replacement. It disables btrfs checksums and compression for those files. Stop the Gateway and other database users, keep verified backups and enough free space, and check any required extended attributes or security labels before replacing a store. Managed updates defer this optional rewrite by default. Eligible older databases can reclaim deleted pages with an initial downtime and temporary-space cost. Automatic full integrity scans have stopped; requested quick checks provide different, narrower corruption coverage.

Background session and archive maintenance retains its limits while joining command shutdown and respecting denied database access. Message commands keep their action result when a plugin shutdown hook times out; deferred cleanup can remain unfinished. The Control UI and healthy agents can become ready while eligible large agent stores finish checking, but those agents stay unavailable until admitted.

Node diagnostics add main-isolate heap details, reply-size measurements and approximate heap changes for RPC handlers. Memory samples exclude overlapping handlers and expose their coverage; a missing sample is unavailable coverage, not zero use. These measurements help investigate memory pressure without claiming an exact allocation count.

See [restart recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery#safety-valves-and-observability), [Gateway diagnostics](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/diagnostics#rpc-response-size-and-heap-changes), [Prometheus](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/prometheus), [Windows](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows) and [message commands](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/message).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Idle browser nodes can update while upload files are retained, with restart recovery for interrupted cleanup. [#154384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154384). Thanks [@xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Older compacted sessions can enter enabled cold storage after the configured idle period while references to required history remain protected. [#157994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157994). Thanks [@anyech](https://github.com/anyech), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keeping a deleted agent’s history no longer blocks the affected plugin-store cleanup during Gateway shutdown. [#158473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158473). Thanks [@anyech](https://github.com/anyech), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Doctor can remove abandoned update files beside unrelated Node or Bun apps when readable process and package evidence confirms they do not use those files. [#160158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160158). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored).
- A restart can skip repeated database scans after idle or finished writers close successfully, even when other shutdown cleanup reaches its deadline. [#160201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160201). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateways already running this fix retain cleanup code through package replacement and save final speech with heuristic notes; model notes can be regenerated after restart. [#160674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160674). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Busy database writers move automatic checkpoint work away from saving changes, addressing one source of pauses on large stores. [#160818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160818). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cloud workers retain their new runtime after a Gateway update until installation is recorded, avoiding a missing-runtime failure on the next turn. [#160820](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160820). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible scheduled session cleanup runs in the background and preserves publication records belonging to a recreated session. [#160858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160858). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New request errors distinguish a Gateway restart, shutdown or temporary pause and explain when to try again. [#160869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160869). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Linux btrfs stores gain in-place-write preparation and offline Doctor conversion; this disables filesystem checksums and compression and requires the documented tools, free space and retained backups. [#160877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160877). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Running and queued work can survive live connection-policy changes when its original access remains valid; clients still reconnect, and actual access loss still cancels affected work. [#160909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160909). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A delayed node-connection alert is suppressed when its pairing check finishes after Gateway shutdown begins. [#160915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160915). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session-history cleanup can resume after its blocking writes are safely checkpointed even while unrelated writes continue; relevant open readers still postpone cleanup. [#161061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161061). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Active session updates can commit while background maintenance performs its initial database check. [#161181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161181). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Docker package JSON receipts no longer leave an extra copy of the finished archive in the shared npm cache; initial packing can still use that cache. [#161548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161548). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shutdown and restart close shared native workers and their supervisor after accepted work finishes; cleanup failures remain visible. [#161558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161558). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automatic five-minute and daily full database scans stop; requested quick checks provide less coverage for index and page corruption. [#162015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162015). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway shutdown cancels future background maintenance and waits for running callbacks before releasing resources; stopping one subsystem leaves unrelated schedules active. [#162079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162079). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shutdown retains native worker cleanup errors after the supervisor exits; terminal cleanup failure requires a process restart before that source can accept new work. [#162128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162128). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Orderly shutdown waits for accepted database maintenance before closing storage; emergency process-exit cleanup remains best effort. [#162166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162166). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automatic session cleanup stands down during Gateway shutdown, avoiding repeated maintenance-worker startup; later writes can resume cleanup after restart. [#162212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162212). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Deleting an agent or running path-scoped maintenance now closes its session cleanup worker when the state directory is reached through a symlink. [#162248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162248). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway shutdown keeps the database worker available to finish accepted memory writes, reducing repeated worker starts without discarding writes or shortening shutdown deadlines. [#162616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162616). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Once a stop or restart signal is accepted, the Gateway immediately refuses new work while continuing its existing shutdown and restart policy. [#162679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162679). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agent deletion and state-root cleanup through symlinks can release execution and history resources retained by archive pruning, preview and related storage maintenance. [#162700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162700). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- macOS service diagnostics read the selected Gateway job’s own state and report unknown when launchd output cannot identify that job. [#162838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162838). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cleanup can release a worker lease after first database creation even when read permission was revoked before its receipt arrived; the revoked operation still fails and leaves the session unchanged. [#162950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162950). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Maintenance errors identify the operation that first lost access and distinguish storage failure from unavailable ownership. [#163103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163103). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Database heartbeat errors retain ownership-loss details already received before a worker fails. [#163134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163134). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Cold-storage maintenance selects inactive archives in workers while protecting active sessions and retaining existing limits. [#163200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163200). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Archive retention runs ordinary database cleanup in workers and rechecks changed references and files before deletion; retention rules stay the same. [#163218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163218). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saving restart and update notices moves off the request thread while required saves remain enforced. [#163364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163364). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Optional orphan-result cleanup checks small idle batches after restart and provides fixed Git operation names for diagnostics. [#163445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163445). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restart recovery checks durable session stores in workers and rejects stale or cancelled results. [#163457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163457). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Control UI and healthy agents can become ready while eligible large agent databases finish checking; pending agents remain unavailable until admitted. [#163690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163690). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Built-source memory diagnostics add an isolated workload and snapshot reference paths for investigating retained objects. [#163764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163764). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Pending agent database checks return retry hints and keep startup probes waiting while healthy agents and the Control UI remain available. [#163839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163839). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway logs identify the client requesting a valid chat or session stop, including fast requests previously missing attribution. [#163851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163851). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sandbox permission edits avoid unrelated service restarts; slow service cleanup stays isolated and required restarts retain bounded shutdown grace. [#163931](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163931). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Worktree cleanup and recovery use the owning Gateway; uncertain outcomes require inspection and sandbox recreation requires exclusive offline ownership. [#163953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163953). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Node memory diagnostics expose heap-space metrics and before-and-after heap-profile breakdowns for the main V8 isolate. [#164057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164057). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed-delivery health counts avoid unrelated history scans without planner statistics, with a one-time writable index rebuild. [#164131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164131). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Docker CLI containers preserve fresh Gateway ownership across process namespaces and explain how to share the namespace or run TUI inside the Gateway container. [#164236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164236). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@JakeMalis](https://github.com/JakeMalis).
- Gateway diagnostics measure reply sizes and approximate memory changes; later exclusive sampling excludes overlapping handlers. [#164335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164335). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restarts wait for accepted subagent cleanup, and eligible agent-database preparation can start before unrelated subagents finish restoring. [#164403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164403). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- RPC memory samples exclude overlapping handlers and report sample coverage; an omitted sample does not mean zero memory use. [#164418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164418). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Container ownership recovery uses proven process death or a heartbeat older than 90 seconds, protecting verified live owners. [#164546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164546). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Alex-vonAllmen](https://github.com/Alex-vonAllmen), [@coygeek](https://github.com/coygeek).
- Doctor and gateway start can re-enable a verified disabled Windows Gateway task, with profile-aware guidance and foreign-launcher refusal. [#164728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164728). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@taoxia255-del](https://github.com/taoxia255-del).
- Message commands keep their action result when a plugin shutdown hook times out, with unfinished shared-state cleanup reported as deferred. [#164915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164915). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Crash-loop safe mode preserves interrupted-turn retries and resumes replay after the full stable window, unless another crash or shutdown intervenes. [#166238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166238). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@KoalaShen](https://github.com/KoalaShen).
- Temporary database snapshots survive module reloads; shutdown waits for readers and stale cleanup cannot remove a replacement snapshot. [7b64dada](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7b64dadaeeb43212fb23787feaf8722f56a45032). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Configuration-triggered Gateway restarts continue after duplicate file-watcher notifications while retaining readiness and secret checks. [a4cc77c3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a4cc77c355f50dc650087815b43a2b73a34c29ae). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Pending session-maintenance callbacks stop when database access is refused, avoiding unhandled errors without changing the restriction. [5ca95288](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5ca95288927072852018ac363be57bb5154032a6). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway inspection recognizes Bun watch and hot commands and separate Node heap-limit values; worker diagnostics omit runtime stack fields. [4c7d05d9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4c7d05d917325b77836455a55837ecb2b1b23eef). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Overlapping session-recovery requests read after the preceding recovery is saved, avoiding false conflicts while retaining access checks. [2a206318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2a206318a7f53bfaa0aa9112c21aed405ac6ab16). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and temporary agent commands wait for their background database maintenance before releasing access or removing storage. [b58e6e98](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b58e6e98933e82d956cad7991c3df4c312665f8c). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update, Doctor and delivery ownership timers handle backward clock changes and distant deadlines without expiring prematurely. [15405665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1540566502bde1656f525ef4f2b9283d1d310379). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Messaging

Telegram albums and forwarded messages can stay together when matching items are queued on a busy host, while follow-ups and completed replies retain their place in the conversation. The channel fixes also cover attachments, voice-only answers, Discord controls and setup changes that need attention during an upgrade.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Telegram media and progress">

Telegram can keep a photo album or forwarded burst in one turn when matching items are already queued on a busy host. Collection remains bounded, so items that arrive after the batch closes can still form another turn. Successive messages from the same sender can also join the queue behind a busy agent, avoiding timeout-related replays and accidental merging while waiting.

Uncompressed PNG and JPEG files reach the existing image-understanding pipeline, and long tasks can show completed progress messages even when a reply plugin disables raw previews. Those messages still pass through the plugin. Progress links avoid website preview cards, while final answers retain the account's preview preference. See [Telegram messaging](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/messaging) and [media understanding](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/media-understanding).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Completed Telegram progress blocks can appear during a long single-agent task with reply plugins applied, while explicit streaming and block opt-outs remain effective. [#161546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161546), thanks to [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [@sercada](https://github.com/sercada).
- Telegram rich messages turn safe numeric `tg://user?id=` links into named mentions when sending or editing. [#162523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162523), thanks to [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@alexph-dev](https://github.com/alexph-dev), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

**Bug fixes**

- Telegram routes supported image files to image-capable agents without requiring users to resend them as compressed photos; PDFs remain documents. [#141292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141292), thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@sercada](https://github.com/sercada).
- Hyphenated plugin commands remain complete and copyable in Telegram command help, including across list pages. [#141332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141332), thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
