- When Telegram cannot delete a replaced media message, it attempts to clear the old inline controls; controls can remain if the edit also fails. [#159012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159012), thanks to [@wakqasahmed](https://github.com/wakqasahmed), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- A guarded preview from a replaced Telegram turn cannot overwrite the current turn's message after a scheduling delay or flood wait. [#160137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160137), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Deeply nested Telegram rich replies render excess nesting as readable text with media source links instead of overflowing the call stack. [#160437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160437), thanks to [@Ayushdevo](https://github.com/Ayushdevo), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@hpyhandsome](https://github.com/hpyhandsome).
- Eligible forwarded attachments are buffered before download, keeping their note and context together during slow downloads; retryable download failures leave the burst pending. [#162787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162787), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram forwarded text and media can combine across separate polls within a one-second quiet window and the five-second batch deadline. [#163286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163286), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram progress links suppress website cards without changing the account's final-answer preview preference, including when progress and final text match. [#163727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163727), thanks to [@ivan-magda](https://github.com/ivan-magda).
- Photo albums queued behind a busy agent retain their chat or topic arrival order, avoiding retries that could replay or misplace photos while waiting. [#163926](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163926), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram message-tool sends, including native inline buttons, work after affected ACP or chat handoffs without an invalid inferred reply target. [#165137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165137), thanks to [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).
- Same-sender Telegram messages and forward batches can take separate places in the queue while the agent is busy, avoiding timeout-related replays and accidental merging while waiting. [#164549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164549), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram holds album and forward collection for matching members already queued under local backlog. Normal quiet windows remain 500 ms for albums and one second for forwards; extra album backlog holds stop at 20 seconds and forwards retain their five-second batch deadline. A newly joined album item still gets its quiet window, and later arrivals can form a separate batch. [db966d6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/db966d62ae0fc6ec3da692dfd5dee3707e8a5b7a), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Related proposal: [#163863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163863); later import of the same change: [52c49fd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/52c49fd2f02016283b29f0114bff8931510fffba). Both are contextual sources.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Attachments, text and voice replies">

Replies with several quoted attachment paths now deliver each file in order, including compact lists and filenames with spaces or commas. Voice-only answers using a valid paired speech block also complete when the model includes reasoning, without requiring filler chat text. The [TTS commands guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tts/commands) explains that reply format; the [tool-profile guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tts/api#tool-profiles) shows how to allow the TTS tool in a restricted profile.

Remote-worker attachments are read from the worker's actual files, including completed live replies. Raw paths must stay inside the worker's assigned workspace: copy outputs from places such as `/tmp` into that workspace before attaching them. Unreadable attachments produce a labeled failure instead of silently substituting a local file.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- TTS documentation shows `tools.alsoAllow: ["tts"]` for restricted tool profiles, with separate [automatic speech-output guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tts/output#auto-tts-behavior); restricted profiles do not gain TTS by default. [#155950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155950), thanks to [@fede-kamel](https://github.com/fede-kamel).

**Bug fixes**

- Completed subagent work sent through the direct-message fallback retains attachments, captionless media and voice-note intent when the requester cannot be awakened; a partial terminal send is not automatically repeated. [#142852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142852), thanks to [@Finn763](https://github.com/Finn763), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@hotfemale](https://github.com/hotfemale).
- Several whitespace-separated, fully quoted paths on one legacy `MEDIA:` line deliver as separate attachments in order, preserving spaces and punctuation in filenames. [#150958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150958), thanks to [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Plain-text outbound replies preserve comparison text following affected emoji or astral Unicode letters instead of removing it as apparent markup. [#160850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160850), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Outgoing structured channel data retains an own field named `__proto__` when internal runtime text is removed and the result is serialized. [#160938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160938), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord and LINE preserve fenced code across long messages when newline-based streaming chunks are selected. [#162412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162412), thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Newline chunking with a zero or negative limit keeps a fenced block and its following paragraph together. [#163255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163255), thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Comma-separated, quoted `MEDIA:` paths deliver every attachment in order while retaining punctuation in names. [#163448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163448), thanks to [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313).
- Compact `MEDIA:` arrays retain every listed attachment and its order, including filenames containing spaces or commas. [#163627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163627), thanks to [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313).
- Replies using carriage-return-only or mixed line endings retain attachments, captions and literal fenced content. [#163665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163665), thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Newline chunking preserves Unicode line and paragraph separators inside fenced or inline code. [#163697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163697), thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
- A selected zero or negative media limit uses the existing bounded fallback instead of rejecting every buffer, restoring affected Matrix buffer sends. [#163701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163701), thanks to [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Remote-worker attachments use the remote bytes and enforce the assigned-workspace path boundary, including in completed live replies. [#164219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164219), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A nonblank paired `[[tts:text]]` speech block counts as a complete answer when the model also returns reasoning, allowing supported inbound channels to deliver audio without visible filler text. [#164285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164285), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Twilight-Networks](https://github.com/Twilight-Networks).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Queued messages and reply recovery">

A follow-up queued while the agent is busy can finish after the request that queued it has closed, and canceling a reply while model settings are loading releases the conversation for the next message. Eligible stalled channel requests can receive one continuation from saved context; a matching queued follow-up can answer both requests. Completed answers already sent to the source chat no longer trigger a false stalled-turn retry notice.

Delivery recovery continues to distinguish an unsent message from one whose delivery is unknown. If an old Reef send has no reliable receipt, check with the recipient before resending. The [queue guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) covers how follow-ups are handled.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Queued follow-ups resume after their normal quiet period when the system clock moves backward. [#160836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160836), thanks to [@nwang783](https://github.com/nwang783), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Eligible Control UI and WebChat background-command or restart replies return to their own conversation instead of the configured heartbeat destination; failed restart replies remain retryable. [#161006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161006), thanks to [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@oywino](https://github.com/oywino).
- An eligible stalled channel request can attempt one continuation from saved context. Web UI and group-thread requests need a matching queued follow-up, and the continuation can still fail. [#161069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161069), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Channel-reformatted replies retain the original conversation association needed for queued delivery and completion tracking. [#161123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161123), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Successful background media requests can acknowledge the request and later deliver the result without a false missing-reply or yield-failure notice while waiting. [#161154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161154), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Canceling a reply during model-settings discovery releases that reply so it does not block the next conversation message. [#161319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161319), thanks to [@Baumus](https://github.com/Baumus), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Custom channels using the direct drain with `deferredLaneOccupancy: release` can admit the next same-lane message when a handler immediately defers. [#161476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161476), thanks to [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
- Invalid Telegram topic zero is rejected before sending; use a positive topic ID or omit the topic for an ordinary chat. [#162197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162197), thanks to [@adamczhang](https://github.com/adamczhang), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Model-timeout recovery after compaction can use an already-saved user turn without attempting to append it again. [#162260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162260), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- When a turn ends after its successful source-chat progress batch, OpenClaw avoids a redundant final-answer request. [#162367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162367), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- A waiting follow-up cannot replace the original question during that question's existing model retries or fallback; the follow-up receives a separate turn. [#162439](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162439), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- A sent final reply keeps its completion result and sent notification if notification for an earlier suppressed reply fails; the earlier error remains reportable. [#162509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162509), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An answer already sent to the source chat suppresses a later false stalled-turn recovery notice; progress and partial sends do not count as completed answers. [#162756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162756), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Deliberately silent unauthorized group commands avoid false missing-delivery warnings. [#163148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163148), thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Early Reef sends without canonical receipts retain unknown delivery status without imposing a historical-rejection cooldown. Check with the recipient before resending; later recorded rejections retain the guarded rephrasing rules. [#163205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163205), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation delivery records are written in database workers in the recorded send order, and a saved reply remains consumed even if the waiting caller later expires. [#163222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163222), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Affected channel listeners can continue sending replies after the maintenance work started on host wake has closed. [#163268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163268), thanks to [@scotthuang](https://github.com/scotthuang), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@ChenGuoShun1979](https://github.com/ChenGuoShun1979), [@Harvey-XuH](https://github.com/Harvey-XuH).
- Unsupported actions on a loaded channel identify the unsupported action in the diagnostic instead of saying the plugin is not loaded. [#163551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163551), thanks to [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@axeg0](https://github.com/AXEG0).
- A definite rejection before dispatch no longer produces a false uncertain-delivery notice on the next turn. [#163769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163769), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepared sends on channels without a preparation callback can finish after an unrelated plugin reload when their registration and settings remain unchanged. [#163893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163893), thanks to [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Channel approval completion forwards cleanup, including completed Telegram approval IDs. [#163969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163969), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completion records for message-tool-only turns are written in database workers, retaining the existing best-effort persistence behavior. [#164112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164112), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation reset or deletion waits for already-started channel processing to settle after queued cancellation; an idle canceled turn releases promptly. [#164247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164247), thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Durable-session retry and recovery checks read the original submitted messages in database workers. If comparison evidence is unavailable, chat retries return a retryable unavailable response rather than replaying an unverified answer. [#164313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164313), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Replies without a session key retain the session-ID and file fallback without attempting invalid keyed lookups or draining keyed events. [4a1c078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4a1c07851faa9aa7fb50bc8de4f08e3134949e88), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delayed queued follow-ups can complete after the triggering request closes instead of repeatedly failing against its closed work lifetime. [3400fd9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3400fd992cfce289f33e25271eaecfc7362e7e8d), thanks to [@xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen). Related proposal: [#148025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148025) (context).
- Shared channel message processing waits for session metadata to finish writing before dispatch, avoiding a first-use database initialization race. [4324af4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4324af413b0606667368b063814f49a598db8af9), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retrying an affected canceled chat request returns its recorded timeout even after its reservation was lost; changed input using the same request ID still conflicts. [9b83d35](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9b83d350f1841d632f28fbb24153669e330a36be), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Discord threads, models and voice">

An agent allowed to read a Discord parent channel can discover that channel's active threads without a server-wide wildcard. Explicit thread denies still apply. Discord's model picker also accepts long configured model IDs while preserving the full model selected when you press Submit, and voice joins and playback use elapsed-time deadlines that tolerate system-clock changes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Discord voice joins retain their connection allowance when the system clock changes. [#156979](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156979), thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Parent-filtered Discord thread discovery returns only permitted children and their member records, honoring explicit child denies; Discord's guild-wide inventory still enters the trusted adapter before filtering. [#160629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160629), thanks to [@PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@postoso](https://github.com/postoso).
- Discord attachment uploads retain the caller's mention restrictions when component delivery falls back to classic messages. [#161604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161604), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord and OpenAI voice codec handling validates numeric and allocation inputs and cleans up partially initialized codec instances. [#162592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162592), thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Discord playback deadlines tolerate system-clock changes, avoiding cutoff or delayed cleanup caused by that trigger. [#163652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163652), thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
- Discord audits honor the canonical `allowFrom` list, including an explicit empty override; deadline failures remain timeouts during synchronous cancellation, and Doctor names `dangerouslyAllowNameMatching` correctly. Very old voice-TTS or guild-channel settings require the 2026.9.7 Doctor bridge described below. [#163728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163728), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord's picker shortens display labels for model IDs over 100 characters while retaining the exact full selection through Submit. If the catalog no longer resolves the selection, reopen `/models`; shared prefixes can still produce identical displayed labels. [#164311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164311), thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Slack, Matrix, WhatsApp and other channels">

Slack fallback progress cards use quieter narration by default, with detailed tool activity available through the existing option. Slack and Matrix previews hold ambiguous fragments of the internal silent-reply marker until the text resolves. Matrix also stops a contact-mapping update when reading existing account data fails, protecting unrelated contacts; retry that repair after account-data reads recover.

WhatsApp named accounts use their own group activation settings. If a group relied on the old shared setting, run `/activation always` or `/activation mention` in that group using the intended account. Doctor also preserves populated account maps instead of adding an unlinked default that changes which account receives commands.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- The [Nextcloud Talk guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/nextcloud-talk) explains message receipt, permission checks and reply delivery separately, so a webhook acknowledgment is not mistaken for a completed answer. [#157715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157715), thanks to [@deepujain](https://github.com/deepujain), [@jm9151821](https://github.com/jm9151821).
- The optional [IMAP plugin](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/imap) fetches pending message bodies in batches of 20 during catch-up, preserving ordered retries without retaining every body at once. [#160413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160413), thanks to [@Ayushdevo](https://github.com/Ayushdevo), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@addyCooks](https://github.com/addyCooks).
- Slack fallback cards use quiet narration by default. Set `channels.slack.streaming.progress.toolProgress: true` for detailed activity; when explicitly disabling it while keeping fallback cards, also select `style: card`. [#161472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161472), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Bug fixes**

- Slack and Matrix draft previews hold a possible lone-`N` silence-marker fragment while ordinary words beginning with N and a genuine final N remain deliverable. [#160582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160582), thanks to [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@imabotone-ui](https://github.com/imabotone-ui), [@nashtu3000](https://github.com/nashtu3000), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Automatic channel resolution accepts Slack workspace-qualified user IDs without the `--kind user` workaround. [#160619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160619), thanks to [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Tlon can process a renewed group invitation after the preceding invitation ends, under its existing auto-accept and inviter rules. [#161596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161596), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A failed Matrix account-data read stops the direct-room mapping write instead of erasing unrelated contacts. [#161727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161727), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix silently declines excess requests when its pending pairing queue is full instead of sending an empty code or false approval reminder. [#161767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161767), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mattermost interaction callbacks containing JSON `null` return the invalid-body response instead of throwing a context exception. [#162603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162603), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- WhatsApp named accounts no longer inherit the old unscoped group activation setting; rerun the desired `/activation` command in the intended account's group when needed. [#162799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162799), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reef's model guard accepts the exact `gpt-6.1-sol` ID under its existing validation rules. [#162955](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162955), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tlon displays configured model names such as `constructor` and `toString` as ordinary strings. [#163438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163438), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix caps new-pairing cooldown tracking and returns the original whitespace text when a compatibility chunker receives a nonpositive limit. [#163740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163740), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Twilio removes an empty stream-ID registration on disconnect so the next inbound call can stream. The ngrok loopback option still requires valid Twilio signatures. Very old Mattermost `allowPrivateNetwork` aliases also lose their Doctor converter; preserve the configuration and resolve that obsolete shape before upgrading. [#163767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163767), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves existing channel account maps and WhatsApp shared policy. It warns about ambiguous unlinked defaults for operator review instead of deleting them; preserve needed shared policy before explicitly removing an unwanted default. [#163974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163974), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Matrix partial previews hold punctuation-prefixed silence-marker fragments such as `.NO_REP`; divergent text resumes streaming and genuine final fragments remain deliverable. [#164259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164259), thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Channel setup and upgrades">

Fresh Telegram, Feishu, Microsoft Teams and Nextcloud Talk webhook installations use Gateway routes by default. Existing operated installations retain their historical listeners through explicit `legacyWebhook` pins. Move the external callback or proxy to the Gateway route and verify delivery before removing a pin, and retain `meta.migrations.webhookListeners` so it is not recreated. Historical Teams and Nextcloud listeners can retain wildcard network binds. Upgrade core to at least **2026.9.9** before manually replacing these standalone plugins.

Telegram configurations that contain a full `/bot<TOKEN>` endpoint instead of an API root now require `openclaw doctor --fix` and a restart; custom proxy prefixes are retained. Read-only configuration sources need a manual correction. For very old channel state or settings, keep a **complete pre-update backup** and follow the [old-version upgrade guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#upgrading-very-old-versions) and [Doctor migration guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations). Some retired formats require an intermediate release before this version can start. In particular, the Matrix JSON-state bridge has shared-database compatibility constraints: do not run an older release against an already upgraded database.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Telegram troubleshooting distinguishes the Node networking threshold from OpenClaw's maintained [supported-runtime requirements](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/node). [#159240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159240), thanks to [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
- New installations use Gateway webhook routes, while existing eligible webhook accounts retain explicit historical listener pins. Telegram keeps the old listener until replacement `setWebhook` registration succeeds; move and verify callbacks before removing pins, and upgrade core to at least 2026.9.9 before standalone plugin replacement. [c7c937a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c7c937abfea22f17be5cac24bfc906d4d91368f6), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete). Related proposal: [#162759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162759) (context).

**Bug fixes and migration changes**

- Doctor imports implicit shared-root WhatsApp credentials into `credentials/whatsapp/default` with private `.migrated` backups and conflict preservation. After manual replacement, run `openclaw doctor --fix`, upgrading core first if needed. Explicit `authDir` stays authoritative. Remove the reported ignored `exposeErrorText` key, including false values, or use its documented 2026.9.5 Doctor bridge. [#163203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163203), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Nextcloud Talk preserves and refuses retired replay-deduplication JSON files; migrate affected state with 2026.9.5 Doctor in the same profile before upgrading. [#163216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163216), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix preserves and refuses active `thread-bindings.json` and `startup-verification.json` files instead of importing them. Complete migration before upgrading and keep a compatible pre-update backup; the 2026.9.5 bridge cannot safely be assumed to open a newer shared database. [#163292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163292), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord preserves and refuses old model-picker-preference and thread-binding JSON files. Use a pre-update backup and the 2026.9.5 Doctor bridge before upgrading. [#163329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163329), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Zalo Personal Doctor repairs supported older direct-message keys for named agents without converting genuine groups; run `openclaw doctor --fix` for affected state. [#163399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163399), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Normal updates back up and repair older Telegram SQLite polling offsets before startup. Manual replacements need `openclaw doctor --fix`; sidecar-only historical state needs a complete pre-update backup and the 2026.9.5 bridge. Replace retired `groupMentionsOnly` with `groups["*"].requireMention` through the documented migration path. [#163418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163418), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retired Matrix trusted-DM, flat private-network and room/group allow settings, and Slack channel allow settings, require the 2026.9.5 Doctor bridge. Retired Discord voice-TTS provider fields and guild-channel `allow` or `agentId` require **2026.9.7** Doctor before upgrading; current replacements use `voice.tts.providers`, channel `enabled` and top-level bindings.
- Old unversioned Telegram reply-cache entries become cache misses until observed again; supported versioned entries and conversation history remain available. [#164173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164173), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram reports full bot-endpoint configuration and directs operators to Doctor repair before startup, probing or sending instead of silently trimming the URL. [#164414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164414), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor skips inferred historical Nextcloud Talk listeners when the channel is absent, incomplete or disabled, avoiding a startup refusal on installations that do not use it. [96622c4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/96622c4447ed8c2df5a297fa8d6da49709ae0e19), thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retired Nextcloud Talk `allowPrivateNetwork` configuration requires its documented 2026.9.5 bridge before adopting the current webhook plugins.

</details>
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Memory

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Scoped memory and saves before compaction">

Compatible memory plugins can now supply records to Active Memory, project recall, Memory Wiki and realtime voice when they support the capabilities each reader needs. With a native provider selected, OpenClaw determines whether a request can use the owner's private memory or memory from one exact conversation. Spawned agents inherit a validated scope, and a parent reset or replacement invalidates the old access. Older spawned sessions without the required access record need to be respawned from their parent. Children whose parent predates reset tracking need to be respawned after the parent's next reset.

Participating plugins can also save important context into their own storage before a conversation is summarized, including from a read-only workspace when source permissions and tool policy allow it. This save is best effort—a failed or skipped save does not stop compaction. Memory Core keeps its existing file-based behavior. Provider status reports the selected provider's health and labels the separate Memory Core data; search and deep index diagnostics refuse to inspect that data as though it belonged to the selected provider.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Let compatible record-based providers serve supported recall, Wiki and voice readers, with selected-provider diagnostics [#162175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162175). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Determine private or exact-conversation memory access in the host and validate spawned-agent inheritance [a9f6758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a9f675839a0d7bdbcef9a4371513f2f8c91a1d88). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Allow supporting memory plugins to save context before compaction with permitted provider tools [32339db](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/32339db16b0d4af32b9925e46c59f660ec816d42). Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Search and recall">

Search and Dreaming no longer treat distinct multilingual notes as duplicates just because they share an English word. Actual duplicates still collapse. Memory Wiki also preserves accented and non-Latin words in multi-word queries. Active Memory recognizes supported Brazilian Portuguese questions about earlier conversations and tries a deeper lookup after weak initial results in its default mode.

After an embedding service outage, a later search can return to the recovered primary without restarting the Gateway or rebuilding the index. Recovery attempts have a 30-second cooldown and require the provider, model and settings to match the saved index. A working compatible fallback stays in use. The [session search guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-search), [Active Memory lookup explanation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/active-memory/how-it-works) and [recall setup guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/active-memory/enabling#remember-across-conversations) explain keyword searches and when earlier conversations can be recalled.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Clarify when configured recall runs and how to enable remembering across conversations [#161179](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161179). Thanks [@yashas-13](https://github.com/yashas-13), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@Raven-Gt](https://github.com/Raven-Gt).
- Make ordinary Conversation Summary notes eligible for Dreaming while filtering raw conversation turns [#161363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161363). Thanks [@Irish-Joseph](https://github.com/Irish-Joseph), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@Gabrielnkl](https://github.com/Gabrielnkl).

**Bug fixes**

- Ask for a keyword retry when a conversation search is blank [#129120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129120). Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@brainatworkharris](https://github.com/brainatworkharris).
- Recognize supported Brazilian Portuguese retrospective questions for deeper Active Memory lookup [#135137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/135137). Thanks [@wangyan2026](https://github.com/wangyan2026), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@wave-workflow](https://github.com/wave-workflow).
- Avoid repeated full-index scans for filename and path searches [#152585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152585). Thanks [@sahilsatralkar](https://github.com/sahilsatralkar), [@personaltrainerkoichan](https://github.com/personaltrainerkoichan), [@aron-intframe](https://github.com/aron-intframe) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Guide the assistant to use successful configured recall without treating an absent optional Wiki as a failure [#157089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157089). Thanks [@LLagoon3](https://github.com/LLagoon3) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keep accented and non-Latin words in Memory Wiki multi-word searches [#159683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159683). Thanks [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx) and [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Resume search after the configured embedding provider recovers from an outage [#163936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163936). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@cknzraposo](https://github.com/cknzraposo) and [@hubofvalley](https://github.com/hubofvalley).
- Preserve distinct multilingual memories in search and Dreaming selection [#164062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164062). Thanks [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415) and [@jarimustonen](https://github.com/jarimustonen) for the fix and acknowledged prior work.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Conversation compaction">

Long conversations using a context-engine plugin that delegates to built-in compaction can finish several summarization stages without being cut off by one timeout for the whole sequence. Each model request still has a deadline, and stalled plugin work remains bounded. Plugin authors must forward the original host abort signal, as described in the [context-engine guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture-internals/context-engines).

Automatic compaction now honors the configured reasoning level, and its context estimate counts the active summary and conversation tail instead of superseded history. Configured AGENTS.md excerpts also stop at unrelated top-level headings so appendices do not crowd out later requested rules. Summary guidance asks the model to retain completed checks that found a problem and distinguish them from remaining remediation. See the [compaction settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-agents/heartbeat-compaction-and-streaming), [compaction policy](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/session-management-compaction/compaction) and [overview](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Estimate active conversation context without recounting history already replaced by a summary [#150623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150623). Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@BillBOT33](https://github.com/BillBOT33) and [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
- Honor the configured compaction reasoning level during automatic summaries [#159476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159476). Thanks [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@rtfcv](https://github.com/rtfcv) and [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
- Improve summary instructions to retain completed checks that found a problem and separate remaining remediation [#161293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161293). Thanks [@itayzit](https://github.com/itayzit) and [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Keep requested AGENTS.md sections separate from unrelated appendices while preserving fenced examples [#162735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162735). Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco).
- Restore active-context estimates in affected Bun transcript checks and missing Cloudflare plugin artwork [#163051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163051). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Refresh the timeout between built-in summarization stages reached through delegating plugins [#163636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163636). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Indexing and maintenance">

Large conversation histories can catch up and refresh searchable memory without hitting the “too many SQL variables” limit. A separate fix lets affected large sessions finish indexing on rollback-journal storage, including reported Docker host bind mounts, instead of repeatedly failing with “database is locked.” Indexing, forgetting and cleanup also move more database work into background workers, reducing work on the thread serving Gateway requests. Forget still preserves source transcripts and keeps selected sessions excluded from later indexing. An uncertain Forget result requires an explicit retry.

Memory and skills watchers use a 30-second polling default. Explicit `CHOKIDAR_INTERVAL` settings override it, with a 20 ms minimum; a short override increases background scanning. File edits can take the chosen interval plus scan and debounce time to appear. Native event watching keeps its existing timing. Watcher status exposes the active mode and fallback reason; after repairing the native backend, restart the Gateway to retry native watching. Eligible stored Codex OAuth accounts can also supply OpenAI memory embeddings without a separate API key. Account access still applies, and the separate Sign in with ChatGPT token-sharing grant does not authorize embeddings.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Run saved conditional instructions' database work in a background worker [#161395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161395). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse watcher paths to avoid redundant work when processing file changes [#161495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161495). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move memory maintenance planning and forgotten-session reads into workers [#161741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161741). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save generated search-vector cache entries in a worker while checking that the index is still current [#161831](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161831). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Count and prune cached embeddings in a worker, yielding between batches [#161884](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161884). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Finish Forget checks and writes in a worker, retaining previews and requiring explicit recovery after an uncertain result [#161959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161959). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move Forget preview and apply index scans into the retrieval worker [#162045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162045). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Introduce a 30-second polling default and expose fallback health; a later fix restores explicit shorter overrides [#162271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162271). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read source hashes and reusable cached embeddings off the Gateway thread during indexing [#162528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162528). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move durable-session memory accounting and side-chat history preparation into workers [#163045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163045). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scan session-memory startup statistics in the indexing worker [#163194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163194). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Discover stored conversations for indexing, archived search and selective forgetting in the history worker [#163234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163234). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plan durable recall and Dreaming cleanup in the history worker [#163269](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163269). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Skip unnecessary embedding-cache eviction scans and remove only the oldest excess entries [#164083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164083). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

**Bug fixes**

- Share Bedrock instance-role credential lookup during indexing while allowing later credential rotation [#161164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161164). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@slinkoff](https://github.com/slinkoff).
- Stop excluded system-only sessions from falsely marking the memory index dirty [#161834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161834). Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@alfred429](https://github.com/alfred429).
- Keep unresolved-session Forget dry runs from creating a missing memory database [#162155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162155). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore memory catch-up, updates and cleanup for large session selections [#164336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164336). Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309) and [@nkarkare](https://github.com/nkarkare).
- Fix the rollback-journal indexing deadlock that kept affected large sessions out of search [#164624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164624). Thanks [@triciah73](https://github.com/triciah73) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Split and retry oversized Zhipu embedding requests when its 64-item limit is returned in Chinese [00c09b7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/00c09b79fc0f440c390e67c5540d0099137fb0ba). Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Select eligible stored Codex OAuth credentials for OpenAI memory embeddings [f6883b3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f6883b3771c0a40d7bd62fcb99815dcbbb2e24f3). Thanks [@astra-openclaw](https://github.com/astra-openclaw) and [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Upgrading older memory installations">

Doctor no longer automatically imports the four pre-July Dreaming journals in `memory/.dreams/`—`daily-ingestion.json`, `session-ingestion.json`, `short-term-recall.json` and `phase-signals.json`—or the old Wiki state in `.openclaw-wiki/source-sync.json` and `.openclaw-wiki/import-runs/<runId>.json`. It preserves those files and warns when it cannot establish the corresponding SQLite state. Ordinary Markdown memories remain supported. Before upgrading an installation that relies only on these JSON files, preserve a backup compatible with its current version and establish a recovery plan. A verified recovery route for users with no newer backup or established SQLite state is still missing; moving the old files aside does not migrate their data. See [memory guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory) and [upgrading very old versions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#upgrading-very-old-versions).

Doctor also stops automatically changing the old Wiki `bridge.readMemoryCore` setting to `readMemoryArtifacts`, and stops warning about retired Active Memory `plugins/active-memory/session-toggles.json` preferences. An old Wiki configuration can fail validation without the former repair guidance. Check these settings and preserve the old preferences before upgrading; the removed warnings provide no replacement recovery procedure.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Upgrade changes**

- Retire automatic imports of four pre-July Dreaming JSON journals while leaving their bytes intact [#163334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163334). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retire automatic imports of the two older Memory Wiki JSON formats while preserving current SQLite rollback support [#163391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163391). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Remove the old Wiki bridge-key repair and retired Active Memory toggle warning [#163667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163667). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Skills

Agents can find useful installed skills when a task needs them and then read their complete instructions, without needing the whole library in the prompt.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Installed-skill discovery">

[Installed-skill search](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills#search-installed-skills) is available in supported embedded-agent, Code Mode and [Codex sessions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness/runtime-behavior). It can match names, descriptions and capabilities described in instruction text when the agent has permission to read it. Search covers eligible installed skills, including ones left out of the prompt, and may read only part of a long instruction file. Agents can then request the complete instructions by name. Search results alone do not grant permission to install or run a skill.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Eligible installed skills remain discoverable when the prompt's size limit leaves them out of its skill list. Disabled skills and those unavailable for model use remain excluded. [#158089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158089). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@eltonwf-del](https://github.com/eltonwf-del).
- Installed-skill search returns matching names and descriptions, while an exact-name read returns complete instructions or an explicit size error. New instruction reads support files up to 256 KiB. [#158090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158090). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@eltonwf-del](https://github.com/eltonwf-del) and [@Jarvis-Baba](https://github.com/Jarvis-Baba).
- Embedded agents, Code Mode and Codex gain the installed-skill search and read tools. Codex reads use the correct workspace or delivered sandbox copy and return complete instructions or an error. [#158091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158091). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@eltonwf-del](https://github.com/eltonwf-del).
- Search can find capabilities described inside skill instructions under the agent's current native read permission. Removing that permission also suppresses cached or in-progress instruction matches. [#160538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160538). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Agents receive guidance to search for task-relevant skills even when their prompt contains no skill list. Simple conversation can skip discovery, and known skill names can be read directly. [#162842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162842). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Exact capitalization takes priority when matching skill names, so a request for `Deploy` can distinguish it from `deploy`. [#162849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162849). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Long local instruction files contribute their beginning to search instead of losing all instruction-text matches. Coverage remains partial, with up to 16 KiB per file within the total search budget. [#162856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162856). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Discovery guidance also works when filtering leaves only notices or an empty skill list. Existing notices remain visible, and the guidance reflects the tools the agent is allowed to use. [#162963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162963). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- If selected instructions and supporting files exceed the combined 8 MiB limit, the agent receives an explanation to select fewer skills even when diagnostics are off. [#164012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164012). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

</details>
</Accordion>
<Accordion title="Availability, installation and refresh">

Local and Git installs reject unusable skill sources before copying them, preserving the working skill when a forced replacement is invalid. On POSIX systems, [sandbox skill copies](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/sandboxing/workspace-access) can refresh from a read-only OpenClaw installation without a manual permission repair. [Skill troubleshooting](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/skills#commands) also separates skills that were found, skills whose prerequisites are met and skills visible to the agent.

Two older formats need a manual update when upgrading.

- In `SKILL.md` and `HOOK.md`, move legacy `metadata.clawdbot` fields to `metadata.openclaw` so prerequisites, installation hints and hook events continue to load. If both blocks exist, keep the canonical block and merge only the legacy fields you want.
- Move or reconcile legacy `.clawdhub` lock and origin records into `.clawhub`. Existing `.clawhub` records should be preserved when reconciling the two directories.

For development setups, unchanged watched files no longer trigger false restarts that can make skills disappear. Existing `CHOKIDAR_INTERVAL` overrides now also apply to Skills and Memory polling. The default remains 30 seconds, with a 20 ms minimum, so a short override can increase background scanning.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- After the next agent turn, `/context list` and verbose diagnostics report the skill catalog actually included in the prompt. Counts now reflect omitted catalogs and prompt overrides. [#145775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145775). Thanks [@tianhaotian](https://github.com/tianhaotian), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@adembektas](https://github.com/adembektas), [@aron-intframe](https://github.com/aron-intframe) and [@bulingbuling688](https://github.com/bulingbuling688).
- Troubleshooting documentation distinguishes a discovered skill from one whose prerequisites are met or whose instructions are visible to the agent. [#146951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146951). Thanks [@Frosmans](https://github.com/Frosmans).
- Local and Git installs fail clearly on unusable or oversized skill roots before copying files. An invalid forced replacement leaves the existing valid skill in place. [#160492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160492). Thanks [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@suninweb](https://github.com/suninweb) and [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Skill scanning skips more irrelevant and temporary files while preserving file classifications between partial scans. If the classification cache fills, the next scan rediscovers files. [#161881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161881). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Refresh cleanup removes stale sandbox skill copies while protecting mount targets and symlink targets outside the managed copy. [#162295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162295). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- On POSIX systems, sandbox skill copies refresh from read-only installations and existing copies repair on the next full sync. Source permissions, executable files and the guest's configured read-only access are preserved. [#162304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162304). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- SDK resource matching treats a root-only `/build` exclusion as root-only, allowing skills under a nested path such as `deploy/build` to remain discoverable. Anchored exceptions retain the same root scope. [#162455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162455). Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Legacy `metadata.clawdbot` is no longer read in skill and hook files. Migrate the needed fields to `metadata.openclaw` manually to retain prerequisites, installation hints and hook events. [#163104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163104). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Legacy `.clawdhub` records are no longer read and need manual reconciliation into `.clawhub`. Equally matched nodes now require an exact node ID instead of the old-client tie-break, and the April 2026 macOS PATH-audit exemption is removed. [#163965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163965). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unchanged watched content avoids false development restarts, and Claude catalog watchers let one-shot commands exit. Skills and Memory polling honor `CHOKIDAR_INTERVAL`, with a 30-second default and 20 ms minimum. [fcdd870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fcdd87021745fd8352e362626b29b7a2e3a2c87d). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

</details>
</Accordion>
<Accordion title="Workshop and paired-device skills">

Skills on [configured paired devices](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/file-transfers#workspace-skill-installation) can be installed and managed on the device that owns them, with the required workspace read and write permissions. Remote instructions and supporting files also come from the correct host when two computers use identical paths. A cancelled or revoked replacement can restore the old skill, though rollback is not guaranteed if a stuck worker must be forcibly stopped.

Workshop reviews can inspect and revise a returned pending proposal by its ID. The proposal stays pending until it is separately accepted. Compatible Codex sessions also gain a scheduled-review path restricted to host-provided tools within the captured Workshop directory, without shell access, external MCP tools or delegation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Workshop retires a pending review when its activity check throws instead of leaving it stuck. Queued skill selections also retain the choices made when submitted, and selector text counts toward the existing size limit. [#161316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161316). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed remote skill instructions and supporting files are read from the correct host, including when local and remote paths match. A stopped node returns a failure instead of stale local content. [#161440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161440). Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scheduled Workshop reviews add a compatible Codex path using host tools within the captured Workshop directory. It refuses to run when restrictions cannot be verified or required host tools are missing, and excludes shell, process and native tools, MCP, delegation and hooks. [#162406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162406). Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Configured paired-node sources own skill installation, tracking, ClawHub updates and removal under the required workspace permissions. Replacement can restore the previous skill after cancellation or revoked access, with limited rollback after a forced worker stop. [#162960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162960). Thanks [@Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186).
- Personal skill-library browsing, revision lookup and selection preparation load their records without doing database work on the main Gateway thread. New selections check current permissions and exact revisions, while existing sessions retain access to their pinned revisions. [#164165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164165). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Background reviews are guided to list pending proposals and use a returned proposal ID to inspect or revise one. The result remains a pending proposal, awaiting acceptance. [#164292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164292). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible Workshop runs that allow silence can end with `NO_REPLY` after tool rejections have settled, without being prompted for an unnecessary closing reply. Runs requiring a visible response retain reply recovery. [4b08683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4b0868363fab28d689ae7459d15c0aeaf893bd91). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>
</Accordion>
<Accordion title="Skill guidance and commands">

Skill guidance directs general integration requests toward existing ClawHub plugins and explicit skill requests toward skills. Telegram also preserves its built-in session export command when a skill uses the same name, while multiline skill command text keeps its interior indentation and paragraph breaks.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- The tmux skill's completion-marker example uses a fresh randomized marker split across `printf` arguments, so an echoed command or old output does not look like a completed task. Callers need to adopt the updated example. [#146329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146329). Thanks [@GodBlf](https://github.com/GodBlf), [@mrzeepek](https://github.com/mrzeepek) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Feishu wiki guidance uses node listing for a known space or parent and direct lookup for a wiki token, avoiding the unavailable search action. [#161755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161755). Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram keeps the built-in `/export_session` command. A colliding skill receives `/export_session_2` or another unique suffix, so its command alias changes. [#163149](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163149). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- ClawHub guidance searches existing plugins for general integrations and skills for explicit skill requests. Installation still requires separate authorization. [#163848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163848). Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Multiline skill-command payloads preserve interior indentation, tabs and blank paragraphs when text begins on the command line. Whitespace already transformed by a channel remains subject to that channel's handling. [#164151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164151). Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

</details>
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Native Apps

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Organize conversations on Mac">

The experimental macOS native sidebar brings conversation organization, assignment and sharing into one place. Give conversations an icon and color, arrange groups, assign an owner, and copy a Markdown transcript, conversation link, preview or durable ID. Opening a new window keeps the intended Gateway, agent and conversation. Editor shortcuts require a resolved local working copy, and available actions depend on the connected Gateway, your permissions and the conversation's state.

Follow-up trees and cross-agent Pages keep related work within reach. Filters, grouping and sorting are saved per Gateway profile, while detail cards show available people, workspace, branch, pull request and progress information. Load more reaches older threads, and search combines local results with connected Gateway search. Snooze removes eligible conversations from Active while their work continues, with messages or run completion able to wake them.

On the experimental native sidebar, use Command-click or Shift-click to select visible root conversations, then mark them read, create or change groups, archive, restore or confirm deletion. Drag conversations to organize groups and Pages. Gateway write permissions apply, and pin placement and ordering require administrator access. Successful changes remain if others fail. Archive Undo lasts six seconds, pauses while hovered or focused, and expires on reconnect.

[Plugin session catalogs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/webchat#session-catalogs) also bring supported source conversations beside OpenClaw threads in the selected-agent view. Sending the first message adopts a source conversation. Deleting one requires the source's support, write or administrator permission, and confirmation that another runner is not using it. The identity card provides Gateway selection and Dashboard, Settings and Usage for that window's connection. See the [Mac chat guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/webchat) for the native controls.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Full Mac chat windows show consistent conversation facts, immediate renames and reconciled late or failed updates across navigation surfaces. [#161745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161745). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Experimental Mac conversation rows show clearer names, status, channels, descendant summaries and supplied previews, with Pin and Archive controls. [#161973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161973). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac Online people cards show reported activity, environment and conversation links, with other-viewer counts excluding yourself. [#162022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162022). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac settings changes preserve supplied permission and tool guards and reject unsupported capabilities before sending. [#162043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162043). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Apple child lists retain larger reported totals and available partial rows. Custom Swift child-list callbacks must return `OpenClawChatChildSessionsResult(rows:isComplete:)`; old array overloads can yield empty defaults. [#162278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162278). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac conversations gain icon and color organization, owner assignment, transcript and link copying, and correctly targeted new windows. Deleting a group retains its conversations. [#163001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163001). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac Load more reaches older selected-agent threads; local and Gateway search retain results and expose retry or incomplete-index notices. [#163114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163114). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac group defaults select a starting Gateway folder and eligible separate Git working copy for new conversations; failed saves retain selections for retry. [#163162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163162). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac filters, owner views, grouping, sorting, previews and reset choices persist per Gateway profile. [#163164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163164). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Full Mac chat detail cards expose available people, workspace, branch, pull request, progress, notepad and preview information, with last-known notices when needed. [#163165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163165). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac sidebar draft markers and queued-message attention counts accompany More actions for the selected conversation when the Gateway UI supports it. Only draft presence and attention counts are shared. [#163193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163193). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac eligible root conversations gain Snooze and Wake, preserving existing pins while messages or run completion can wake them. [#163241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163241). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac More actions waits for the selected conversation menu to render and cancels a late popup after switching. [#163351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163351). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Abandoned Mac conversation-menu requests finish promptly without a misleading timeout or late popup. [#163672](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163672). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac follow-up trees, expandable agent rosters and cross-agent Pages retain loaded children and use configured image avatars with text fallback. [#163699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163699). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Experimental Mac visible-root multiselection adds batch actions and drag organization, retaining successful operations through partial failure. [#164013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164013). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Experimental Mac archive Undo and selected-thread group creation preserve successful results; restored threads return to Active and cross-agent names remain distinct. [#164073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164073). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac plugin catalogs add project or person grouping, owner filters, profile-specific visibility, independent paging and retry, and supported source actions. [#164436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164436). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac sidebar identity and Gateway menus route Dashboard, Settings and Usage to the window’s Gateway. The request badge counts the oldest request kind rather than all kinds together. [#164591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164591). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connect and navigate on Mac">

Mac chat stays open while the same Gateway finishes setup or reconnects. Opening a completed conversation can recover a failed Dashboard, and an unrelated saved-profile refresh no longer interrupts sign-in on an unchanged connection. Switching the primary Gateway clears its former menu-bar conversation and preview cache. Replacement notifications also stay visible when an older dismissal animation finishes.

Eligible dashboards protected by Cloudflare Access can sign in inside the app and retain an app-specific browser session. This uses the observed HTTPS site on port 443 and the same Access site, team and account; browser restrictions can still require the app's sign-in link.

Saved remote Gateways can reconnect after a certificate renews when its hostname is valid and macOS trusts it. Learned pins follow system trust, including administrator-installed roots. An explicit `gateway.remote.tlsFingerprint` remains fixed and must be updated deliberately; expired, untrusted and wrong-host replacements remain rejected.

For automation, `--no-activate` can present chat or Dashboard windows behind the active app. LaunchServices also needs `open -g -n`. Actions needing new permissions, interactive Keychain access or other prompts are deferred; relaunch without the flag and retry them. Paired Mac discovery of Claude Code conversations also respects `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` from the app's own launch environment.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Opening a completed conversation restores a failed Mac Dashboard; queued native actions can recover after a canceled replacement load. [#160886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160886). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A replacement Mac notification stays visible when an older dismissal animation finishes. [#161250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161250). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved Mac Dashboard sign-in survives unrelated profile refreshes on the same active connection. [#161464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161464). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Eligible Cloudflare Access dashboards support in-app sign-in and persistent app-specific browser sessions. [#161474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161474). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac automation launches can avoid foreground activation, with prompt-requiring actions deferred until a normal relaunch. [#163758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163758). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Paired Mac Claude Code discovery respects nonblank `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` in the app launch environment, otherwise using the home-directory default. [#163787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163787). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac menu-bar conversation and preview caches follow the primary Gateway, clearing former data on a switch. [#163892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163892). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac chat stays open while its unchanged Gateway completes setup or reconnects, including command-line chat launches. [#163971](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163971). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac learned certificate pins accept hostname-valid renewal trusted by macOS, including saved-Gateway opening and switching; explicit fingerprints remain fixed. [#164435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164435). Thanks to [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@joryirving](https://github.com/joryirving).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Run local Gateways on desktop">

A fresh packaged Mac setup can start its included local Gateway through This Mac without a separate Node installation, CLI installation or administrator step. The app also supplies the runtime and SQLite support for its private worker and browser setup. Fresh local Gateways stop with the app by default. Enable Keep OpenClaw running when the app is closed to keep an app-managed Gateway's channels and automations running in the background; changing this setting leaves a paused Gateway paused.

Eligible older default-profile app-managed Node services can migrate in stages to the bundled runtime, with a separate recovery path for eligible paused default or named profiles whose service is absent. Independent installations, operator runtime pins and channel policies are excluded. If needed settings exist only in the service definition, first save them in the profile configuration or `.env`, run `openclaw gateway install --force`, then retry. Installs using the current bundled CLI also refuse when a newer service or runtime choice appears during installation, so inspect that choice before retrying. Retained older CLI recovery paths are outside this protection. The [bundled Gateway guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/bundled-gateway) explains these cases.

Fresh Linux companion setups use bundled Bun. Existing Linux Gateways retain their runtime choice until you confirm Use bundled runtime… in the tray. The Gateway must be running, and intervening service changes refuse the switch. Failed activation offers manual recovery with no automatic rollback. Returning to Node uses `openclaw gateway install --force --runtime node`. See [Linux runtime adoption](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/linux#adopt-the-bundled-runtime) and [Bun compatibility](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun-compatibility).

Mac and Linux packages use the final shared Bun update, including fixes for plugin-created files, module paths, child-process messaging and stack formatting. The earlier Mac runtime updates are consolidated into this package update; it does not switch separately managed Node installations.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

- The Mac private worker and browser setup use bundled Bun and SQLite support without separate Node or Homebrew SQLite. [#161603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161603). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fresh This Mac setup starts the included Gateway for the app lifetime, while eligible existing installations retain their ownership. [#161709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161709). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Mac startup dependency-target mismatch is corrected while the macOS 15 minimum remains. [#161754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161754). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@coygeek](https://github.com/coygeek).

- An earlier bundled Mac runtime update improves compatibility when copying large files. [#161825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161825). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
- The Mac x64 worker avoids a native process-reader startup crash under Rosetta. [#161876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161876). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- An earlier bundled Mac runtime update fixes streams retaining their parent process and adds module-loading and process-callback compatibility. [#163081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163081). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- An earlier bundled Mac runtime update fixes output shutdown, worker messaging, preload ordering and canceled network requests. [#163687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163687). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The bundled Mac runtime sequence fixes affected package loading for hook-using plugins and tools; the hook-free hosted Gateway was unaffected. [#163831](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163831). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Remote or unconfigured Mac profiles without a local service avoid false uninstall failures. A loaded service whose definition was removed outside the app is not stopped by this path. [#164069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164069). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
