</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Voice, attachments and chat state">

On iOS, paste copied images or screenshots into an attachment-enabled composer and remove them before sending. File URLs copied from Files are outside this image-paste support. On Mac, a delayed file selection or drop cannot attach files to a conversation selected afterward, and Apple link previews retain the title and image for their current URL.

Voice fixes keep replaced or canceled capture attempts from interfering with the current one. Mac push-to-talk ends on right-Option release and cannot start later from a stale permission response. Canceling it does not send; a normal release can briefly wait for final speech results. Android dictation restarts survive late cancellation from the previous attempt. iOS Voice Wake preserves queued stereo input, while native Apple realtime playback preserves opening words and ordinary audio bursts through brief microphone delays.

Apple chat also keeps late compact, fork, rewind and settings-save results from replacing another thread's visible state, and preserves case-distinct conversation IDs. Watch reply recovery avoids an unnecessary retry pause when reconnect cancels its old history request.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Mac push-to-talk retires the correct hold on key release, permission delay or voice-mode replacement. [#143064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143064). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- iOS Voice Wake preserves both channels of queued stereo microphone audio when the capture buffer is reused. [#161148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161148). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restarted Android dictation keeps listening when an older permission or recognition attempt is canceled late. [#161391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161391). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iOS pastes copied images as removable attachments in enabled composers; Files URLs are not included. [#161627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161627). Thanks to [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- iOS Talk removes directives from cumulative text, repeated Voice Wake commands work after listening restarts, and retired health checks cannot publish stale failures. [#161639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161639). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Late Apple compact, fork and rewind results cannot replace newer conversation errors or history. [#161799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161799). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Apple local-state storage avoids duplicate closing after rejected initialization and concurrent database misuse. [#162782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162782). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Apple transcripts keep case-distinct Matrix, Signal and catalog conversation keys separate while retaining structural aliases. [#162818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162818). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Apple realtime voice playback preserves opening words and ordinary bursts through brief microphone delays. [#163146](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163146). Thanks to [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez).
- Apple requires explicit stored-token retry permission, and late microphone permission cannot revive a canceled or replaced voice note. [#163433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163433). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A failed Apple settings save from a departed thread cannot replace the current thread’s visible errors while its queued messages are put on hold. [#164037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164037). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delayed Mac file selections and drops stay bound to their original conversation, and Apple link previews show only their current URL’s title and image. [669db5d36e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/669db5d36e0637ba4c3033028ef050303963715b). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Watch reply recovery starts promptly when reconnect cancels an old history request, avoiding its unnecessary retry pause. [287a4003d3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/287a4003d34f14a3e3422456944bd6e1e6edb8ed). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Mobile conversations and test builds">

iOS and Android add Snooze and Wake controls for eligible conversations, plus a Snoozed view. Snoozing leaves work running, and new messages or completed runs can wake a conversation. iOS can browse cached Active and Snoozed lists offline; archived lists and mutations need a connection. See [iOS conversation controls](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/ios#sessions).

Both mobile apps support reactions on eligible saved messages, with counted chips and accessible emoji controls. Changes arrive live without extra notifications. Reaction actions respect permissions and exclude unsupported archived, source-catalog or unsaved messages. On iOS, an initial identity lookup failure can leave your own reaction highlighting and removal unavailable until reconnecting or switching sessions. See [iOS reactions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/ios#reactions).

Android group changes and confirmation dialogs remain tied to the Gateway where they began. Switching Gateways dismisses stale rename or delete dialogs, and late responses cannot change the new connection's errors. iOS demo and screenshot modes hide real saved Gateway and credential controls.

The Android daily-build pipeline supports Firebase test delivery after Play upload and continuation of a failed Firebase stage. Phone and Wear communication tests need the same distribution channel because signing differs. Release operators need the mandatory Firebase preflight before building or uploading to Play.

Apple and Android connection retries now require explicit permission to use a stored token. Update an older Gateway first if it relies on the retired retry fallback. Android also retires older inbound event aliases; dependent older Gateways need updating. iOS and its share extension use the `openclaw-ios` identity.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- iPhone and iPad demo captures hide saved Gateway and credential controls and remove contradictory pairing prompts. [#160857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160857). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android long-press Snooze presets, the Snoozed list and early Wake apply to eligible root conversations while work continues. Pinning or archiving clears snooze. [#162293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162293). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iOS saved-message reactions add counted chips, an emoji picker and VoiceOver controls, with live updates and permission checks. [#162296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162296). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android saved, nonstreaming message reactions add chips, media-message long-press access and TalkBack controls; inline add appears alongside existing reactions. [#162307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162307). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android daily Firebase test distribution supports retained-stage recovery and notification safeguards after Play delivery. [#162371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162371). Thanks to [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- iOS Snooze and Wake add Active, Snoozed and Archived views, including cached Active and Snoozed browsing offline. [#162399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162399). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iOS and its share extension retire old client identities and overrides. Local Talk uses Gateway-configured keys; Gateway-routed credentials stay on the Gateway. [#163339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163339). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android honors explicit stored-token retry permission and denial instead of the retired code-only fallback. [#163347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163347). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android in-flight group rename and delete remain on their original Gateway and reject stale responses. [#163420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163420). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android inbound events use canonical `payload` and `paramsJSON` fields; retired inbound aliases require dependent Gateways to update. [#163441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163441). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android dismisses stale or restored group dialogs on Gateway changes. Deleting a group leaves its conversations Ungrouped. [#163455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163455). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android stops importing the older recent-notification-app suggestion setting while preserving the current forwarding setting. [#163675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163675). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Translations and older settings">

Native translation resources add missing wording across 21 existing non-English locales. The updates cover conversation organization, people and activity, reactions, snooze, archive and Undo, source catalogs, connection recovery and background Gateway guidance. Platform-specific labels remain platform-specific, including Mac primary selection and iOS Primary and Retry now. These updates extend existing language coverage.

Apple apps retire selected preference and certificate imports from before July 1, 2026. If your setup relies only on those older settings, select and reconnect your Gateway and establish certificate trust again.

</AccordionGroup>

## Models and Providers

Model choices refresh without a Gateway restart, and provider and coding-agent sessions retain more of their conversation context.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Model lists and selections">

Downloaded model choices and prices can take effect without restarting the Gateway, at the next catalog check or an explicit model-list refresh. Existing turns keep their previous prices. Catalog preparation also avoids unnecessary native-agent activation and uses less duplicated data and worker memory.

Explicit provider choices now take precedence over colliding aliases. For example, `openai/gpt-4o-mini` now selects OpenAI; use `openrouter/openai/gpt-4o-mini` to retain the former OpenRouter route. Check saved choices because the account, availability and cost can change. Commands with `--agent` now reject changes to shared text or image fallbacks; omit that option when you intend a shared change.

<Accordion title="Model choices and thinking controls">

[GitHub Copilot](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/github-copilot) gains bundled Claude Sonnet 5.5 and Opus 5.5 definitions, subject to account access. Supported Ollama Cloud models and Codex reply routes retain maximum thinking, and eligible Daybreak Blue and Red accounts regain their higher effort choices.

New Baseten setups start with DeepSeek V4.1 Flash. If your saved Inkling choice is no longer available, select a replacement manually. The meaning of Off remains model-specific. For GLM 5.3 and GLM 5.3 Flash on Ollama it selects the lowest supported reasoning level; for xAI models without effort controls it leaves native reasoning in place.

<Accordion title="Ultrafast selection">

Ultrafast is available as an explicit choice on eligible OpenAI Responses routes, including direct API-key setups. Codex uses it only when you select it through the shared speed controls and the account's model catalog supports it. Unsupported selections fall back to Fast. See the [Codex speed controls](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness/commands#shared-fast-mode-and-codex-fast-mode).

The requested tier is subject to the provider's account and model limits. Custom Responses endpoints now receive `service_tier` from saved Fast settings or authored tier settings and may reject that field; check compatibility when using a custom endpoint. A requested tier does not establish the speed delivered or the price charged.

- [Offer Ultrafast with direct API keys and require an explicit eligible selection in Codex](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164556) — Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Provider accounts and connections">

[Bedrock](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/bedrock) picks up externally rotated AWS profile credentials without a restart. Provider login and cancellation fixes also preserve selected configuration, avoid unused connections, and report self-hosted credential-storage failures before setup claims success.

For ZAI usage checks, use supported configuration, the auth store, `ZAI_API_KEY` or `Z_AI_API_KEY`; the old Pi auth-file fallback has been removed. If Ollama Cloud still points at `https://ai.ollama.com`, set `models.providers.ollama-cloud.baseUrl` to `https://ollama.com` yourself. Doctor no longer corrects or warns about that old cloud address.

Developers embedding multiple Node model runtimes can give each one separate credential and request policies, connections and cleanup through `createNodeLlmRuntime`. Explicit runtimes need those policies supplied by the embedder and require Node asynchronous-context support.

<Accordion title="Agents API sessions and tools">

Hosted Agents API attachment turns retain usable originals and give clearer feedback when uploads cannot continue. Attachment steering is queued as a full follow-up. The [runtime guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes#agents-api-mvp) also explains how to verify actual execution and returned files; hosted machines do not automatically receive your Gateway files or skills.

Native tool settings apply after a Gateway restart and a new or reset session. `nativeTools: []` removes native web search. To also disable programmatic tool calling, set `nativeTools` to `[{"type":"programmatic_tool_calling","enabled":false}]`. Older `authFingerprint` session bindings, including those from 9.7, no longer support continue, reset or delete and have no automatic migration. New-format bindings retain the remote session ID across credential changes, but a hosted session still needs its creating key for access. See the [Agents API guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/agentsapi).

<Accordion title="Generated images, speech and video">

OpenAI OAuth image generation can retry with a configured OpenAI chat model when the account rejects the default. Runway and Together video generation honor longer requested timeouts.

For ElevenLabs speech, choose the model with `modelId`. If your configuration uses the old `model` field, change it manually using the [speech configuration guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tts/configuration) and [field reference](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tts/field-reference#param-model).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Conversation input and completed answers">

Eligible OpenAI HTTP conversations can reuse the previous response across pauses of up to 90 minutes, sending only new input when the retained history still matches. Earlier eviction, expiry or cleanup sends full history instead. Completed Astra answers also survive silent tool follow-ups, with duplicate streamed final answers suppressed.

[Image-handling guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/media-understanding) explains repeated image costs and the tradeoff of replacing selected images with descriptions. Those descriptions remove direct access to the selected pixels for that turn. Custom OpenAI-compatible routes can opt into [stale-output pruning](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-pruning). Code Mode callers should use `awaitResults`, replacing `required`, on `exec`, `shellexec` and `agents_wait`; the old argument is ignored.

<Accordion title="Provider errors and recovery">

Provider failures retain useful retry hints and the original rejection where it helps explain the problem. Malformed or output-limited tool calls can receive one guarded recovery attempt, while local SQLite failures stop futile model switching. Remote Codex launch-approval failures now point to the approval problem without rotating healthy credentials.

A recovery after an output limit keeps completed tool results and leaves the incomplete call unexecuted. Earlier actions may already have completed, so check those results before repeating an operation.

<Accordion title="Claude and CLI conversation continuity">

Eligible direct Anthropic API-key conversations keep a stable cached prompt prefix when workspace instructions change, with updates supplied later in the conversation. Temporary context stops being sent on the next user turn while remaining in the saved transcript. A plugin whose registration failed and was rolled back also stops blocking otherwise healthy agent turns.

CLI agents keep Gateway tools available after the first turn, and Claude CLI fixes preserve context through supported forks, child replies and history recovery. Eligible long local shell work can return a process handle and finish in the background. See [CLI backends](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cli-backends), [Anthropic retained context](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic#tool-calls-and-retained-thinking), and [child tool policy](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/tool-policy).

<Accordion title="Codex delegation and shared context">

Solo Codex conversations can delegate when native admission hooks are unavailable. In that mode, a message from a second signed-in person is queued as a follow-up. Existing conversations affected by the earlier source-main delegation regression need a new conversation. Stale readiness also gets one refresh before a child follow-up is rejected.

Codex connections without the managed relay receive shared workspace persona and memory. Persistent conversations pick up edits on the next turn. These connections omit selected personal `USER.md` profiles; use the managed parent-only relay if you need those personal preferences. Child conversations may inherit shared context. Incognito continuations after native compaction and fresh children can see creation-time instructions before refresh; removing persona does not erase earlier history.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources</summary>

- [Preserve solo Codex delegation when native admission hooks are unavailable](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161070) — Thanks [steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Refresh stale Codex readiness once before rejecting a follow-up child task](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161155) — Thanks [b4rRa](https://github.com/b4rRa), [obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103).
- [Deliver shared workspace persona and memory to Codex connections without the managed relay](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162156) — Thanks [Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186), [mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex sessions and saved history">

Replacement Codex conversations are protected from delayed commands and cleanup belonging to an older session. An interrupted chat can resume while sibling chats continue when interruption and subscription cleanup succeed. Idle agents' catalogs load on request instead of starting catalog servers at Gateway startup, and fleet discovery avoids a configuration-copy path that exhausted memory.

Large saved histories need fewer repeated file checks. If file notifications silently miss an in-place edit, previews and listings can remain stale until a restart rebuilds them. Older schema-1 Codex sidecar bindings are retained for diagnosis but no longer imported; Doctor reports an invalid binding rather than converting them.
