<Accordion title="Prepare remote workspaces and reuse sandbox dependencies">

Node-hosted agents regain permitted Gateway web, memory, session and installed-skill tools while file and process work stays on the node. Remote setup loads less work before admission, and Windows workers can return edits from deeply nested repositories. Supported Linux paired nodes also reduce completion overhead through native helper reuse; update the native node host to receive it.

Eligible new private Docker or Podman worktrees can reuse prepared pnpm dependencies on Btrfs, APFS or ReFS. This requires a committed lockfile, an available immutable image and a verified install; the first generation still installs dependencies. Missing prerequisites or preparation failure fall back to source-only files, and existing sessions keep their own checkout. Slow allocations renew their lease independently, while automatic Git maintenance moves to hourly cleanup or `worktrees gc`. See [node session hosting](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/session-hosting) and [managed worktrees](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Keep new stock persona templates in the configured agent workspace when direct, child or session-bound scheduled work runs in a separate project. The project retains its working directory and AGENTS.md instructions; existing files remain. [#161088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161088). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@nikosml](https://github.com/nikosml).
- Store workspace save and recovery journals in database workers. Wait for durable records before changing files or reporting success; uncertain commits retain files and recovery records. [#161539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161539). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Wait for a cancelled remote worker’s entered workspace changes and process cleanup before replacement work starts. Revoked queued work does not launch; unconfirmed node cancellation keeps replacement blocked while its old environment is stopped. [#161759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161759). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share Git discovery for matching concurrent opt-in observed-project requests. This does not change default project listing, and a slow bulk discovery can still delay unrelated metadata reads. [#162016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162016). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let ordinary hidden native children use separate managed Git checkouts without a persistent sidebar session. Keep retains the checkout; delete snapshots it before removal. ACP and cloud placement still require their supported visible-session paths. [#163124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163124). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep an existing cloud worker’s original session and workspace when main-session aliases change, removing that cause of recovery and Stop failures while new requests still follow current aliases. [#163133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163133). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Store workspace snapshots, aliases, setup updates and expiry cleanup in database workers. Plugin authors can replace beforePersistentApply with a SQLite-free guard.assertHost; the old callback remains supported until the next SDK major. [#163496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163496). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Route worktrees create through the authenticated local Gateway with operator.admin, or an exclusive offline owner. Preserve creation options and require upgrade-or-stop guidance for older Gateways; after an uncertain result, inspect worktrees list --json and the repository before retrying. Other worktree commands retain their existing paths in this change. [#163853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163853). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce the wait after model streaming for supported Linux paired-node replies by reusing the native watchdog connection for workspace-protection renewal and release. Update the native node host to receive this improvement; worker-bundle refresh alone is insufficient, and durability and cleanup gates still apply. [#163866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163866). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce completion overhead for already-running paired-node workers on supported Linux native hosts by reusing an exact-owner idle helper and prepared Git result identity. Update the native host to receive helper reuse; final delivery still waits for durable workspace reconciliation and visible conflict or failure results. [#163954](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163954). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Pause advisory disk probes and repeated warnings while a paired device is offline or session hosting is disabled. Keep its workspace and last sample, then refresh on the next scheduled sweep after reconnecting. [#164048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164048). Thanks to [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@wynxo](https://github.com/wynxo).
- Reject cloud-workspace uploads after the authorizing result loses ownership, and wait for recovery and any started upload before releasing the transfer. [#164104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164104). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Overlap worker-bundle download with runtime installation during Crabbox setup, and stop the owned installer before removing temporary files on failure or cancellation. [#164187](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164187). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore permitted Gateway web, memory, session, installed-skill and GitHub identity tools to node-hosted agents while keeping file and process work on the node. Queue tool calls beyond the four-call transport limit; browser and computer tools depend on placement capabilities. [#164296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164296). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Return Windows worker edits from deeply nested Git repositories using command-scoped long-path support. The repository’s saved setting stays unchanged; other Git clients may still need their own long-path setting. [#164331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164331). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Coordinate in-process setup of the same local workspace through bootstrap and sandbox preparation. Cancelled or revoked queued requests stop before creating files; separate directories remain independent. [#164377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164377). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse verified pnpm dependencies for eligible new private Docker or Podman worktrees on Btrfs, APFS or ReFS with a committed lockfile and an available immutable image. The first generation installs them; failed or unsupported preparation uses source-only files. Existing sessions keep independent checkouts and do not auto-reinstall after their own lockfile edits. Disable preparation with `worktreeAcceleration:false`. [#164517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164517). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Renew managed-worktree allocation independently during slow Git preparation. Fetches skip automatic maintenance, which moves to hourly cleanup or worktrees gc with its own 30-minute timeout. [#164521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164521). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reject changed or missing worker session targets before workspace preparation starts. [be13aee](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/be13aeea2072f847b9a7810bd96631c60a957602). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Load remote turn runtime after admission and tool validation, avoiding preparation for rejected or cancelled work. Verified installations carry its required split runtime files. [4aa24e3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4aa24e3a1340cf17e0d661e87d35d7789948d76f). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Show a worker’s failed status and recovery reason after cleanup completes. Worker commands avoid secret-store reads from temporary local state; separately delegated GitHub credentials remain available when supplied. [2e33b81](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2e33b81f2efa7d4b2a719640b58837fb02e1d814). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Carry independent lease renewal and deferred Git maintenance into the synchronized release branch alongside the worktree change above. [b8f5fea](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b8f5feae579eda0d3a110345bdc6fa5680baf5fd).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Run commands and find useful recovery guidance">

CLI commands avoid loading some unused runtime code. `openclaw gateway call` can connect without validating unrelated settings or preloading plugins; use `openclaw config validate` for a full configuration check. Configuration refusals give usable retry arguments, and common chat, terminal and Control UI errors offer a short explanation with expandable details. Missing tool results remain unknown outcomes, with guidance to inspect current state before repeating actions with side effects.

Other fixes preserve unsent terminal drafts, refresh custom usage footers and keep an already-detailed approval or blocker answer from being replaced by a repeated explanation. See the [Gateway call reference](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/query), [progress cards](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/progress-card) and [tool-loop detection](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/loop-detection).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Reject unsupported process wait arguments with instructions to use `timeout`, keeping output available for a corrected request. [#153844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153844). Thanks to [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Reject unsupported `exec` working-directory arguments before a command runs, with guidance to use `workdir`. [#154248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154248). Thanks to [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Recognize repeated tool calls rejected for invalid arguments when tool-loop detection is enabled, so the agent can correct the request. [#158709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158709). Thanks to [@ali-log](https://github.com/ali-log), [@51Google](https://github.com/51Google), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Preserve unusual keybinding names and their string or array overrides when loading and reloading them. [#161749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161749). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move repetitive recurring-job start messages from debug to trace, keeping useful Gateway diagnostic history longer within existing limits. One-shot starts remain at debug, failures remain errors and scheduling continues unchanged. [#161895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161895). Thanks to [@matthematics1137](https://github.com/matthematics1137), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Honor an explicit session-creation transport timeout in either fallback branch, including inherited spawn policy. Bundled callers using default or null deadlines retain their existing waits. [#161969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161969). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Guide agents to report requested undelivered results, meaningful changes and unresolved problems, while staying quiet about already-handled background results. [#162302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162302). Thanks to [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Keep manual result-collection reminders in running process status, logs and lists when completion notifications were disabled at command launch. Structured followUp and no-exit-wake labels remain until completion; execution and notification policy stay unchanged. [#162303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162303). Thanks to [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Avoid command-cleanup modules for affected version queries and package-root library imports. [#162311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162311). Thanks to [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@love-ai-open-source](https://github.com/love-ai-open-source).
- Preserve unrelated paths when shortening home directories in tool details and CLI diagnostics, including containers whose home is the filesystem root. [#162483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162483). Thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Avoid a false blocked-tool warning when steering skips a pending tool that never started. [#162513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162513). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY), [@dori-jitter](https://github.com/dori-jitter), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Select the intended local transcription program when a home-relative PATH entry passes through a symbolic link and its parent, preserving configured precedence and home directories containing PATH separators. [#162680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162680). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Refresh file-based custom usage footers after repeated editor saves that replace the file, including deletion and recreation. Refresh still depends on filesystem notifications, which an open old-file handle can delay on Linux. [#162733](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162733). Thanks to [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco).
- Preserve intended emoji boundaries on affected Bun runtimes when splitting messages or truncating terminal text, avoiding unnecessary cuts that waste the available space. [#162749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162749). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep POSIX sibling paths containing literal backslashes absolute in CLI diagnostics while shortening actual home-directory descendants. [#162800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162800). Thanks to [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco).
- Guide agents to leave a note with the unfinished task, blocker, owner and resume condition when paused work has no authorized next step. [#162903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162903). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Keep unsent slash-prefixed terminal chat after a disconnected send is rejected, preserve newer drafts and allow retry after reconnecting. [#163201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163201). Thanks to [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Treat a missing tool result as an unknown outcome and guide agents to check current state before repeating actions with side effects. [#163379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163379). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Give common chat, terminal and Control UI errors a short explanation and recovery step, with expandable details. Keep uncertain dispatched outcomes, manual database recovery and Stop-saving warnings visible. [#163381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163381). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Restore specific recovery guidance for disconnected Codex devices, outdated workers and offline runners. [#163538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163538). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explain that an allowed nested configuration parameter is unset and point to config set instead of calling it unknown. The message does not establish provider support for that parameter. [#163682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163682). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cap oversized log-follow intervals so timer overflow cannot turn a long wait into rapid Gateway polling. [#163791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163791). Thanks to [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
- Show the Gateway’s specific error when a visible child session fails startup or registration, including unconfirmed cleanup. [#163798](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163798). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Allow up to 15 minutes of Node Gateway heap sampling when both collected-object flags are false or omitted. Including objects collected by either major or minor GC keeps the 30-second cap; profiles describe allocations made during capture that remain alive at stop, without retaining-path analysis. [#163813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163813). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let Bun source installations launch from external agent workspaces, preserve selected-agent usage reports during unrelated store activity and track admitted child completion through graceful restart or suspension. [#163821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163821). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restore standard MIT license recognition while keeping the same terms and third-party notices available through the README and package. [#163838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163838). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Observe model-preparation failures while the terminal UI is idle, preventing an extra unhandled-rejection report while retaining the original error. [#163842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163842). Thanks to [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Suppress late approval messages after the terminal UI closes. Remove this path’s old ACPX gateway-instance-id/process-leases.json import and legacy api.githubcopilot.com-to-Codex URL rewrite. [#163854](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163854). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Identify dependency packages and frames lacking JavaScript source in heap and CPU profiles while hiding dependency paths, versions and function names. Package and native labels no longer count as fully redacted nodes; unknown frames remain redacted, and native labels do not identify a particular allocator or all external memory. [#163922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163922). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Send literal `constructor` and `__proto__` text to interactive processes even when cursor-key mode is unknown. [#163963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163963). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Identify registered plugin and auxiliary RPC methods by exact name in Prometheus and OpenTelemetry request and timing metrics. Update dashboard queries for registered other-to-name and generic unknown-to-other changes; the shared 2,048-sample cap still drops new series when full and reports those drops. [#164028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164028). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep queued voice, Talk and upload diagnostics attached to the submitting request instead of a preceding task’s trace. [#164105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164105). Thanks to [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Close the temporary agent and shared databases before deleting one-shot agent exec state, including when worker registration finishes late. [#164483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164483). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Preserve a detailed answer that already explains why a progress-card plan needs approval, input or an external dependency. The completion check can finish silently instead of replacing it with a repeated blocker, while authorized unfinished work still receives the check. [#164896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164896). Thanks to [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Start replies in the current session working directory, using the configured default if its override was cleared. [863e377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/863e377524a970787dfb2540b32722810c512cff). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Make Gateway CLI option links jump to their descriptions on the running, query and discovery reference pages. [1edf7ea](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1edf7ea3ab5dd59c9ed6091c030a232504b5a58d). Thanks to [@qingminglong](https://github.com/qingminglong).
- Give `config patch` refusals usable `--replace-path` retry advice, including punctuation and quotes in provider names. Show separate bash/zsh and PowerShell spellings when needed. [e6b3a11](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e6b3a11851de205f5139ea4ea33051409ad36ebb). Thanks to [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Avoid unused CLI startup imports and let `gateway call` check connection settings without validating unrelated configuration. Use `config validate` for a full check. Preserve literal resolved credentials while missing variables still fail, reuse readers for equivalent state directories, and report permission errors after identifying a listener for cleanup. [01d59ff](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/01d59ffc5392541eed49b5c57cad93077c93013a). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Retain less duplicate data during long tool sessions">

Long tool sessions retain fewer duplicate completed results, discarded output strings and finished-request references. Initial history and model context can share equal immutable data, and reusable readers release completed requests without discarding the reader itself. These changes reduce particular sources of retained data; they do not establish a release-wide memory saving.

**Plugin and SDK callers must treat transcript payloads as immutable as soon as they submit them, including while an asynchronous append is pending.** Create new values for later updates. Lifecycle hooks receive the canonical saved activity, including rewrites, with suppressed entries omitted. See the [SDK runtime guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Reduce repeated copying when selecting child sessions that must be protected during maintenance. [#161526](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161526). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Copy only relevant child-run records for control and prompt context, avoiding unrelated tasks in large histories. [#161690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161690). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse database workers on qualified macOS and Linux Bun builds. Stock Bun 1.4.2, Windows and inconclusive SQLite cleanup checks keep conservative worker retirement. [#161764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161764). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Load fewer unrelated modules when starting session, history and usage readers. [#162609](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162609). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share preparation for session updates sent to several clients and avoid repeated secret-filter checks within one output pass. [#162810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162810). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release catalog listeners and disconnected-client caches after stalled discovery or reconnects, while continuing to track genuinely unfinished native work. [#163688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163688). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid copying the old transcript while validating and adopting concurrent history updates. [#163710](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163710). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid rebuilding full session rosters and copying unrelated metadata during lifecycle actions in large stores. Database-worker handoffs can still add to total request time. [#163815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163815). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share immutable published session and worker facts instead of repeatedly copying them for inventory and placement reads. [#164102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164102). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retain less unnecessary data from settled tools, private Code Mode results and published child records during long tool-heavy runs. Persist trajectory events in batches within the existing rolling retention limit. [#164199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164199). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share immutable submitted transcript payloads to reduce duplicate retained tool-result objects. Plugin and SDK callers must treat payloads as immutable as soon as they submit them, including pending async appends, and create new values for updates. [#164251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164251). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release discarded large tool output when a transcript keeps only a short excerpt, including excerpts carried through prepared redaction metadata. [#164361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164361). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release completed read requests and their prompt or transcript data from reusable SQLite read workers while keeping those workers available for later reads. [#164370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164370). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Release duplicate completed tool data, prompt state and finished-run scheduling references in long Code Mode sessions while retaining saved history and the five-minute warm-worker window. Lifecycle hooks receive canonical saved activity, including rewrites and omission of suppressed entries. [#164386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164386). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse frozen configuration captures during reply and tool preparation instead of repeatedly copying unchanged settings. Active turns retain their admitted policy and new work receives updated settings. [#164398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164398). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce nested transcript bookkeeping and share compiled validators for equivalent schemas. Schema contents determine cache identity; an SDK cacheKey no longer selects it. [#164467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164467). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Share immutable equal data between initial session history and model context, including incognito sessions, while keeping private fields excluded from the model’s view. [#164502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164502). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let eligible idle history readers release their database using their recorded capability, and defer the default heap-limit probe until memory diagnostics need it. [9137cfc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9137cfcc5f4e2a3a6cac458590b491c1031b535a). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce transcript-worker imports to the board and progress-card schemas they use, avoiding the broader Gateway protocol dependency. [a02d441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a02d441dbf8aa40a13b6be0a18c5f2b1f15bc77e). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Move routine storage work away from active requests">

More history reads, session updates, usage-cache writes and durable resets run in database workers, leaving less of that storage work on the Gateway thread handling requests. Selected paths also avoid redundant reads, copying and serialization. Saved changes publish after confirmed commits, and known lost replies can be reconciled without repeating their writes. Uncertain outcomes remain pending or fail with recovery guidance. The [worker-access reference](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) describes the covered operations and retained native paths.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Read shared GitHub publication status and recovery records in database workers, reducing storage work on the Gateway request thread. [#161214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161214). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduce repeated database checks for cached state, profile access and session listings while detecting schema changes and revoked ownership. [#161678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161678). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gather cloud-worker idle-suspension candidates in a database worker while retaining the existing suspension policy and pending-recovery exclusions. [#161849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161849). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gather remote-worker disk-monitor candidates in a database worker while retaining the existing probes and disk warnings. [#161878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161878). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resolve requester profiles in database workers for the covered metadata, model-list, search and profile requests, checking current access before effects. [#162414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162414). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep newer upstream-session links intact when delayed monitoring results arrive, preserve retry progress after a failed event write, and continue processing other provider results. [#162518](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162518). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Run conversation archive checks in database workers before writing durable history. [#162521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162521). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gather health and status session summaries in database workers while preserving counts, recent-session order and current store ownership. [#162625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162625). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save remote-worker acknowledgments in database workers before continuing workspace-result recovery. [#162975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162975). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Let the Gateway handle other pending work between session-list preparation batches, while concurrent callers still share preparation and receive the same ordered visible results. [#163031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163031). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read archived-session status in database workers and send restore notifications only after a successful settled restore. [#163041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163041). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Check attachment ownership in database workers and recheck current session access and download tickets before returning protected media. [#163049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163049). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read long histories for recovery, usage totals and MCP App restoration in database workers, publishing prepared messages after commit. [#163053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163053). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepare covered agent and recovery session reads in database workers, rejecting delayed results after cancellation or session replacement. [#163115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163115). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Copy and relink restart-interrupted conversations in database workers while keeping recovery atomic. [#163119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163119). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move completion and reply-preparation transcript reads into database workers, adopting committed messages before notifying observers. [#163120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163120). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid scanning every saved session key when checking an agent-specific store during session creation. [#163163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163163). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepare covered conversation lists, routing addresses and manual-compaction bindings in database workers. [#163208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163208). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save and prune durable usage-report cache data in database workers, keeping refresh locks and known lost-reply recovery without repeating writes. [#163287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163287). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move selected routine session-metadata transactions into database workers and publish after confirmed commits. Lost worker replies do not repeat committed writes. [#163378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163378). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read session versions, bounded history, update watches and notice acknowledgments in database workers without losing queued events during a handoff to the same database. [#163513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163513). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read shared-session suggestions in a database worker and filter them by current access before returning them. [#163546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163546). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read durable pending messages for chat history and startup in database workers, and retain confirmed interruption status after a lost worker reply. [#163582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163582). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move worker-transcript replay bookkeeping into database workers while preserving sequence order and duplicate detection. Unknown writes stay pending for recovery. [#163594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163594). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read saved history for compaction, reset hooks, side questions, exports and asynchronous SDK consumers in database workers. Worker failure does not fall back to a blocking durable read. [#163605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163605). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save supported transcript, queued-input, session and Goal updates together in a database worker. Publish after confirmed commit and reconcile known lost replies once; unknown outcomes stop without repeating writes. [#163889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163889). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keep checking incognito workers when disk-backed session preparation fails, leaving affected disk sessions unknown rather than treating them as absent. [#163972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163972). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Batch database-schema metadata reads instead of repeating queries for every table and index. [#164077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164077). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid serializing unchanged prompt, skill and diff snapshots during routine metadata updates, while fully saving changed or cleared snapshots. [#164093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164093). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Check board membership once per session-list batch instead of once per row, using one committed snapshot for the results. [#164094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164094). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Track conversation-environment activity with one fewer database read per touch while retaining current attachment and ownership checks. [#164108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164108). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Use indexed ownership checks for canonical state handles that have already been admitted, retaining conservative checks for other connections. [#164110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164110). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuse bounded prepared statements on retained read connections while executing queries against current data. [#164117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164117). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Read only needed lifecycle and activity metadata rather than unused saved prompts, skills and diff baselines. [#164125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164125). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Save and settle ordinary pending chat and agent inputs through database workers. Join accepted writes before final outcomes and cleanup; SDK callers can explicitly await completion and settlement without replaying an uncertain write. [#164284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164284). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepare watched-conversation prompt context in database workers and check current watches and permissions. Plugin authors can await the new authority-bound helper; the synchronous helper remains supported. [#164288](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164288). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Record session creation, compaction and child-launch signals, and prune lifecycle history in database workers. [#164367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164367). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delete and conditionally restore saved Codex and Agents API session bindings in database workers, blocking reuse after uncertain outcomes. [#164391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164391). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Move selected chat writer claims, model-switch updates and final-delivery bookkeeping into database workers. [#164514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164514). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reread selected session-creation metadata in a database worker while checking current routing and intervening writes. [#165360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165360). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allow concurrent first transcript writes to create a new session store without mistaking another writer’s creation for a replacement. [922d989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/922d98926bb27143c812be35f9a918c1e48b045a). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoid a repeated database admission read during cold-transcript preparation. [0853228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/08532288490cc7f07543d4254d03fcd5aa1b09d3). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Run durable single-session reset storage in a database worker and publish acknowledged resets after a lost reply without repeating their writes. [850c49c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/850c49ccec1b918e0eaa7aa6fe26f37b527d3f01). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Other Bug Fixes

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Workers, processes and shutdown">

Cancelling a worker chat lets its current turn finish cleanup without starting another provider call. On affected Bun builds on macOS, finishing command output also leaves background processes running when they were intended to continue.

Shutdown attempts worker-tunnel and shared-transfer cleanup even if it cannot read the worker inventory. It still reports the inventory error.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- [Linux streaming commands fail individually when file descriptors run out, leaving unrelated commands running](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151672). Thanks to [@awss1i](https://github.com/awss1i), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- [Restrictive Linux hosts can finish descendant-process cleanup when process-group signals are denied, with matching native host and worker builds](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160465). Thanks to [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- [Moving or dispatching a session to a cloud worker can settle recoverable finished results instead of waiting for a timeout](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160535). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Saving Gateway credentials preserves the paired host launch reply when reads of an unchanged environment are blocked](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160590). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Cleanup remains retryable when a supervisor disappears before its command broker is ready](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161374). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Cancelling a worker chat lets its existing turn finish cleanup without starting another provider call](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163154). Thanks to [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- [Affected Bun builds on macOS leave intended background descendants running after command output completes](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163633). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Shutdown attempts worker-tunnel and shared-transfer cleanup even when worker inventory cannot be read, then reports the inventory error](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164522). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Worker cancellation can finish when the last native operation ends between cleanup checks](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/646a3bdaf60ba92986935d09a36798c2d189bf58). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Linux process checks confirm an exit when a status file disappears between checks, without treating permission errors as proof of exit](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/112df95f5ee65a0b17622735e2b616d2910dc79b). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Saved workspace recovery errors match displayed diagnostics when process start dates contain padded spacing](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d2f875598efa320abe3302077c128ce540390b53). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Linux cleanup recognizes exited processes during kernel reaping while preserving live-thread and permission safeguards](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b57aca41466b11c0f92c1fa3945cfb221185bde2). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Sessions, databases and archives">

Native Incognito deletion works again, and archives stay in the configured store’s artifact directory when the database is reached through an alias. Equivalent paths to the same database also work for pending session inputs and transcript checks.

Temporary database contention before agent work starts gets bounded retries. Snapshot cleanup preserves replacements at reused paths. If the process managing snapshots is lost, unresolved files remain protected until service recovery; restarting it alone does not make manual deletion safe. See the [database integrity and recovery reference](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) before dealing with unresolved snapshot cleanup.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- [A lost reply to a committed category edit no longer invalidates unrelated session reads](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160656). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- [Interrupted database reads retain snapshots still in use until their original owner finishes cleanup](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160946). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Repository sessions retain their committed workspace identity when a reply is lost, and cleanup preserves replacement database files](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161124). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Late cleanup of an old temporary database snapshot leaves a replacement at the same path intact](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161300). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Database contention before a snapshot directory is created reports the original retry error without a false cleanup failure](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161372). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Unrelated database or session-metadata writes no longer discard newly recorded session-state events](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161553). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Database rollback cleanup finishes even when a callback throws, while retaining the original error](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162139). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Temporary database contention before agent work starts receives bounded retries, and a later request can try again after failure](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162750). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@marcoschierhorn](https://github.com/marcoschierhorn), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@produtoramaxvision](https://github.com/produtoramaxvision), [@hannnnn-l](https://github.com/hannnnn-l), [@islandpreneur007](https://github.com/islandpreneur007), [@yaohd100](https://github.com/yaohd100), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- [An unavailable agent database worker is replaced before dispatch, so a pending turn need not be resent](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163258). Thanks to [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hawkit](https://github.com/hawkit).
- [Pending session inputs append correctly when the state directory is accessed through a filesystem alias](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163488). Thanks to [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- [Native Incognito sessions delete successfully, and archives stay in the configured store’s artifact directory when its database path is an alias](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3baeac6d1ffe3322b404d76cde45b25bd84563a9). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Refused snapshot-directory creation keeps its original error and lets other valid snapshots finish cleanup](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/477cab2d714874b0807b8ed7afab7172be8975c7). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Session discovery retains valid Incognito and placement information despite unrelated registry changes or invalid disk candidates](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9cc3eee461ed564ee008f3090702d114e533b949). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Transcript and question-answer receipt checks accept equivalent paths to the same database, including filesystem aliases](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/32d49385f67f09054c8d9b03f10e0e7f3c68d33b). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Commands and platform compatibility">

Native Windows scheduled turns and Control UI chats avoid a session-read error. Windows CLI launches can skip an optional compile cache whose path is too long. An externally set `NODE_COMPILE_CACHE` can still hang before OpenClaw starts; use `NODE_DISABLE_COMPILE_CACHE=1` for that case.

Commands with a literal NUL character now return correction guidance before starting a helper or replacing running work. Valid multiline commands remain supported; see the [exec tool reference](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec). On POSIX systems, `config patch --file` and `config set --batch-file` reject named pipes without a writer instead of waiting indefinitely. Regular files and symlinks remain supported.

Configuration errors include the file path and validation details. If an external edit makes the file invalid while a change is being applied, the request reports an error and keeps the last working runtime configuration. Repair the file, call `config.get`, then reapply the change.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- [Native Windows scheduled turns and Control UI chats avoid session-read DataCloneError failures, including configured session stores](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160075). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@aniketkrs](https://github.com/aniketkrs), [@yashas-13](https://github.com/yashas-13), [@WG-Mojo](https://github.com/WG-Mojo), [@hailongguo0530-alt](https://github.com/hailongguo0530-alt), [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay), [@chiyunshen](https://github.com/chiyunshen), [@jocojolo](https://github.com/jocojolo), [@mick-lu-lingyue](https://github.com/mick-lu-lingyue), [@zouxiao777](https://github.com/zouxiao777), [@zhangming678](https://github.com/zhangming678), [@yihan331313](https://github.com/yihan331313), [@godinpt-floada](https://github.com/godinpt-floada), [@webdood](https://github.com/webdood), [@veritasaiflows-cell](https://github.com/veritasaiflows-cell), [@aron-intframe](https://github.com/aron-intframe), [@Monkeykingll](https://github.com/Monkeykingll), [@avirtudazo-officeconnect](https://github.com/avirtudazo-officeconnect).
- [Pairing readers skip empty records, malformed schema type lists are rejected, and SDK readers preserve existing system-agent approval details](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161054). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Invalid or missing external configuration edits return an application error instead of leaving the request waiting](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161868). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [HTTP dependency updates fix compressed-response and redirect handling while preserving Slack proxy compatibility](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162774). Thanks to [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- [Windows CLI launches and child processes can skip the optional compile cache when its path is too long](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162841). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@NickM83](https://github.com/NickM83).

Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Memory and request cleanup">

Completed chats, old display rows and disconnected tool-event subscriptions release data they no longer need. Short output tails and previews also stop retaining discarded large strings.

On non-Windows Gateway Bash and Zsh paths, the shell-snapshot cache keeps the 128 most recent working-directory and environment entries. Abandoned HTTP responses can release buffers when garbage collection runs; SDK callers should still finish or cancel response bodies for prompt cleanup.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- [Non-Windows Gateway Bash and Zsh shell snapshots retain only the 128 most recent working-directory and environment cache entries](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162967). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80).
- [Old parent display rows release obsolete child data while preserving links and viewer information](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163763). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Completed or hidden chat registrations and obsolete usage records release retained data, and disconnected reads stop preparation retries after admitted work settles](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163834). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Abandoned provider, MCP and plugin HTTP responses can release their retained buffers and readers when garbage collection runs](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164124). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Disconnected clients release tool-event subscriptions without interrupting accepted agent work](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164307). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Short command tails, Memory Wiki previews and web-fetch metadata no longer keep discarded large strings in memory](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164441). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- [Gateway requests unrelated to image routes avoid unnecessary image-handler session-context lookup](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/658e1e292a28fc5ae5781a90e80b0ea6b9cfec6e). Thanks to [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>
